Method: PhysMind是一个无需训练的智能体框架，核心流程包括：(1) 通过物体分割、网格重建和6D姿态追踪恢复时间上一致的动态场景；(2) 拟合解析连续时间动力学和潜在物理参数，无需展开时步模拟器；(3) 根据问题检查、延续或编辑世界，并从生成的轨迹和交互中得出答案。

Result: 在CLEVRER上相比直接CoT推理提升38.23个准确率百分点，在Physion++上提升8.08个百分点；在反事实问题上超过最强评估的VLM基线GPT-5.5达19.25个百分点。

Conclusion: PhysMind通过构建可执行的世界模型而非依赖VLM直接推理，显著提升了视频物理推理的准确性和可靠性，尤其在反事实推理方面表现突出，验证了"世界建模+推理"范式的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable physical reasoning from video requires understanding how objects move, interact, and respond to interventions. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) often struggle to interpret these dynamics and reason reliably about future and counterfactual outcomes. We introduce PhysMind, a training-free agentic framework that constructs one reusable, question-agnostic executable world per video. PhysMind recovers a temporally consistent dynamic scene through object segmentation, mesh reconstruction, and 6D pose tracking, then fits analytic continuous-time dynamics and latent physical parameters without unrolling a time-stepped simulator. Given a question, it inspects, continues, or edits the world and answers from the resulting trajectories and interactions. Relative to direct chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning with the same VLM, PhysMind improves accuracy by 38.23 points on CLEVRER and 8.08 points on Physion++. On counterfactual questions, it exceeds the strongest evaluated VLM baseline, GPT-5.5, by 19.25 points.

</details>


### [50] [ACA-GS: Adaptive-Capacity Anchored Gaussian Splatting for Compact Dynamic Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04581)
*Seunghyeon Song,Joo Chan Lee,Chanung Park,Jun Young Jeong,Minseo Lee,Eunbyung Park,Jong Hwan Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应容量的anchor-based 4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）框架，通过自适应anchor基数（动态调整每个anchor的Neural Gaussian数量）和自适应anchor特征掩码（按区域复杂度分配特征通道），解决了传统anchor-based方法刚性均匀参数化导致的内存浪费问题，在MPEG、Panoptic Sports和N3DV数据集上实现显著存储压缩而不损失视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4DGS技术虽能实现高保真实时时空渲染，但anchor-based方法存在刚性设计缺陷：均匀参数化强制固定Neural Gaussian数量和特征预算，导致复杂度不足区域过度密集而浪费内存。系统需要一种能根据局部时空需求动态分配表示能力的方法。

Method: 提出两个核心机制：(1) Adaptive Anchor Cardinality——根据局部几何或运动复杂度动态调整每个anchor对应的Neural Gaussian数量，在复杂区域集中基元、简单区域抑制冗余；(2) Adaptive Anchor Feature Masking——通过anchor级别的特征掩码调节特征通道，为复杂区域分配丰富特征，为简单区域使用轻量表示。两者协同在anchor-based框架内实现自适应容量分配。

Result: 在MPEG、Panoptic Sports和N3DV三个数据集上实验验证，实现显著存储压缩且不损失视觉质量。在运动复杂的MPEG序列上，相比最先进的anchor-based方法，压缩率提升高达1.5倍，同时保持可比的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过打破anchor-based 4DGS中刚性均匀参数化的限制，提出的自适应容量分配策略有效改善了运动表达力与存储效率之间的权衡，在不牺牲渲染质量的前提下大幅提升压缩性能，为高动态场景的紧凑4D表示提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) enable high-fidelity, real-time spatiotemporal rendering, but expose a fundamental trade-off between motion expressiveness and storage efficiency. While anchor-based designs achieve compactness through anchor-level parameter sharing, their rigid uniform parametrization enforces fixed Neural Gaussian counts and feature budgets per anchor. Consequently, insufficient fidelity is addressed by excessive anchor density, rather than lightweight, targeted increases in Neural Gaussian count or feature capacity, resulting in memory waste. To overcome this rigidity, we introduce an adaptive-capacity anchor-based framework that dynamically allocates the representational capacity based on local spatiotemporal demands. Adaptive Anchor Cardinality varies the number of Neural Gaussians per anchor, concentrating primitives in regions of high geometric or motion complexity while suppressing redundancy. In parallel, Adaptive Anchor Feature Masking modulates anchor-level feature channels, assigning rich features to complex regions and lightweight representations to simpler ones. Experiments on MPEG, Panoptic Sports, and N3DV datasets demonstrate substantial storage reduction without degrading visual quality. Notably, on challenging MPEG sequences with complex motion, our method achieves up to 1.5x higher compression than state-of-the-art anchor-based methods while preserving comparable quality.

</details>


### [51] [MetaVideoAgent: Automated Video-Agent Evolution for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04587)
*Benlei Cui,Ruize Wang,Junjie Li,Jinhao Chen,Longtao Huang,Yinghao Chen,Yuwen Zhai,Jingqun Tang,Ruijian Jia,Weiwei Wu,Pengfei Sun,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetaVideoAgent是一个自动演化视频智能体的框架，针对不同视频分布自动优化设计；同时引入VA-EvoBench基准，经过四次演化迭代将宏平均准确率从38.44%提升至51.47%，且比最强的固定设计视频智能体高出6.39个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要在冗长多模态视频中定位稀疏的、与问题相关的证据。真实视频分布在不同方面（模态信息密度、内容结构、证据模式）存在差异，导致固定的视频智能体设计要么冗余处理，要么因不匹配而失败。将自动化智能体演化从文本扩展到视频面临三大挑战：完整的长时间视频执行使候选验证成本高昂、失败在耦合的证据处理阶段间传播、复杂的预处理与感知工具和定位策略使代码级更新难以可靠实现。

Method: MetaVideoAgent框架包含四个核心部分：(1) 通过稀疏采样帧和相关查询来刻画信息密度和证据需求，从而指导初始设计；(2) 将局部失败压缩为可独立执行的最小验证任务；(3) 构建证据支撑的Gold Paths，审计Student轨迹，跨样本聚合反复出现的失败并归因到负责模块；(4) 采用模块化智能体表示，将每次更新约束在主要负责模块及其必要依赖上。同时引入VA-EvoBench基准，覆盖八个视频分布，每个分布有独立的演化集和留出集。

Result: 在四个视频分布上进行四次演化迭代后，MetaVideoAgent在每个分布上都提升了初始智能体，将宏平均准确率从38.44%提升到51.47%，平均每个分布的演化成本为354万token。演化后的智能体比最强的固定设计视频智能体高出6.39个百分点，同时在所有比较的视频智能体中使用的token和视频帧数最少。

Conclusion: MetaVideoAgent证明了自动化视频智能体演化在长视频理解中的有效性，通过模块化归因和最小验证任务设计，能够以较低成本持续提升视频智能体在目标分布上的表现，并优于固定设计的基线方法。作者将发布所有代码和数据以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding requires locating sparse, question-relevant evidence in long, multimodal videos. Real-world video distributions differ in modality-specific information density, content structure, and evidence patterns, causing fixed video-agent designs to incur redundant processing or fail when mismatched. Extending automated agent evolution from text to video is challenging because full long-video execution makes candidate validation expensive, failures propagate across coupled evidence-processing stages, and complex preprocessing, perception tools, and localization strategies make code-level updates difficult to implement reliably.
  We introduce MetaVideoAgent, a framework that automatically evolves a video agent for a target distribution. It profiles information density and evidence requirements from sparsely sampled frames and associated queries to guide initial design, then compresses localized failures into independently executable minimal validation tasks. It constructs evidence-grounded Gold Paths, audits Student trajectories, aggregates recurring failures across samples, and attributes them to responsible modules. A modular agent representation constrains each update to the primary responsible module and its necessary dependencies.
  We further introduce VA-EvoBench, covering eight video distributions with separate evolution and held-out splits. With four evolution iterations per distribution, MetaVideoAgent improves every initial agent and raises macro-average accuracy from 38.44% to 51.47%, at an average evolution cost of 3.54M tokens per distribution. The evolved agents outperform the strongest prior fixed-design video agent by 6.39 percentage points while using the fewest tokens and video frames per question among the compared video agents. We will release all code and data to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [52] [The First EgoCross Challenge at EgoVis 2026: Cross-Domain Egocentric Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04589)
*Yuqian Fu,Tianwen Qian,Yanjun Li,Yu Li,Kunyu Peng,Xu Zheng,Yongqin Xian,Alessio Tonioni,Yanwei Fu,Xiaoling Wang,Danda Paudel,Federico Tombari,Luc Van Gool,Leyi Wu,Yifan Zhao,Jinjie Zhang,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Wenbo Wang,Yupeng Hu,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie,Takuya Murakawa,Toru Tamaki,Yi Wen,Zhenglin Du,Zhengyang Li,Lingling Li,Licheng Jiao,Wenping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCross是一个跨域第一人称视频问答基准测试，用于评估多模态大语言模型在日常生活场景之外的泛化能力。该挑战赛在CVPR 2026的EgoVis研讨会上举办，覆盖手术、工业装配、极限运动和动物视角四个目标域，共收到超过1500份提交，来自130多名参与者，并公开了排行榜结果和获奖方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型通常在常见日常场景上表现良好，但能否泛化到更专业或非典型的跨域第一人称视频场景仍缺乏系统评估。作者旨在构建一个基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建EgoCross基准，包含四个目标域（手术、工业装配、极限运动、动物视角）的第一人称视频问答数据，每个测试样本由视频片段、问题和四个候选答案组成。设立两个官方赛道：Source-Limited Track（限制使用官方基线和少量支持集）和Open-Source Track（允许更广泛选择但禁止手动构建目标域训练数据）。

Result: 挑战赛收到超过1500份提交，来自130多名参与者，其中19支队伍参加Open-Source Track，38支队伍参加Source-Limited Track。报告公布了官方排行榜和两个赛道的获胜方案总结。

Conclusion: 该报告为跨域第一人称视频理解提供了技术参考，所有资源（包括数据、基线和获奖代码）均公开可用，希望推动该领域进一步发展。

Abstract: EgoCross is a cross-domain egocentric video question answering benchmark designed to evaluate whether multimodal large language models can generalize beyond common daily-life scenarios. The first EgoCross Challenge was hosted at the Third EgoVis Workshop at CVPR 2026 and evaluated models on first-person videos from four target domains: surgery, industrial assembly, extreme sports, and animal perspectives. Each test example consists of an egocentric video clip, a question, and four candidate answers, from which the model must select the correct option. This technical report introduces the challenge task, benchmark resources, and two official Codabench tracks. The Source-Limited Track restricts participants to the official baseline model and a small support set, whereas the Open-Source Track permits broader choices of models and training data under rules that prohibit the manual construction of target-domain training data. In total, the challenge received more than 1,500 submissions from over 130 participants, with 19 teams participating in the Open-Source Track and 38 teams in the Source-Limited Track. We further present the official leaderboard results and summarize the winning solutions from both tracks. We hope that this report will serve as a useful technical reference for advancing cross-domain egocentric video understanding. All resources, including the challenge data, baseline implementation, and code released by the winning teams, are made publicly available.

</details>


### [53] [COSMO: Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation for Source-Free Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04604)
*Bo Li,Junjie Peng,Xiaohua Xie,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COSMO方法，将VLM引导的无源域适应（SFDA）建模为样本级可靠性分配问题，通过锚定共享共识实现源模型与VLM的协同自适应，在四个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFDA在无源数据场景下自生成的监督信号会在显著域偏移时强化源偏差。虽然预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）能提供互补语义知识，但源模型与VLM在不同目标样本上的可靠性存在差异，现有跨模型引导方法未显式建模这种差异，可能导致冲突时覆盖有效的源模型证据，即"源证据遗忘"问题。

Method: COSMO将VLM引导的SFDA形式化为样本级可靠性分配问题。核心思路是用锚定共享共识替代专家间直接引导。具体包括：(1) 构建样本特定的初始共识，偏向更集中的预测分布；(2) 在适应过程中重新聚合两个分支演化中的证据；(3) 基于共识不确定性和训练进度，调节最终共识偏离初始锚点的程度，使共享监督保持锚定又具有自适应性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，COSMO在匹配VLM骨干的条件下取得了最先进的性能。进一步分析表明，COSMO能更好地平衡保留有效源证据与吸收互补VLM证据之间的关系。

Conclusion: COSMO通过锚定共识的协同适应机制有效解决了源证据遗忘问题，在VLM引导的SFDA任务中实现了源模型与VLM证据的合理分配与融合，验证了样本级可靠性分配建模的有效性。

Abstract: Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a source-trained model to an unlabeled target domain without source data, a practical setting under privacy or storage constraints. Yet its self-generated supervision can reinforce source bias under substantial domain shifts. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) offer complementary semantic knowledge, but the relative reliability of the source model and VLM varies across target samples. Existing cross-model guidance does not explicitly account for this variation and may overwrite valid source-derived evidence under conflict, a failure we term source-derived evidence forgetting. We formulate VLM-guided SFDA as a sample-wise reliability-allocation problem and propose Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation (COSMO). COSMO replaces expert-to-expert guidance with co-adaptation through an anchored shared consensus. It first forms a sample-specific initial consensus that favors the more concentrated prediction. During adaptation, COSMO re-aggregates both branches' evolving evidence and regulates how far the resulting consensus moves from its initial anchor based on consensus uncertainty and training progress. This keeps the shared supervision anchored yet adaptive. Across four benchmarks, COSMO achieves state-of-the-art performance under matched VLM backbones. Further analyses indicate that it better balances the retention of valid source-derived evidence with the absorption of complementary VLM evidence.

</details>


### [54] [TRCoRSurg: Temporal-Relational Co-Reasoning for Surgical Video Triplet Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04606)
*Fang Li,Shihao Zou,Weixin Si,Yang Gao,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，通过多尺度编码器提取空间先验，结合标签相关建模模块（MS-CAMRE）和双向时序-关系融合注意力模块（BTRFA），联合建模手术三元组识别中的帧内标签依赖与帧间时序语义，并引入新评估指标TCER来衡量三元组一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术三元组识别方法难以在统一框架中同时建模帧内标签依赖关系和帧间时序语义，缺乏对复杂手术场景中多个相互依赖实体（器械、动作、目标）的联合建模能力。

Method: 1) 多尺度编码器提取类别特定空间先验；2) Label Correlation Modeling模块结合多尺度类别激活图引导的关系提取（MS-CAMRE），捕获静态共现模式与动态上下文依赖；3) Bidirectional Temporal-Relational Fusion Attention (BTRFA)模块协调时序与关系表征；4) 提出新评估指标TCER衡量三元组因果与语义一致性。

Result: 在CholecT45和ProstaTD数据集上达到SOTA性能，AP_IVT分别提升5.1%和7.8%；TCER指标上分别相对降低超过36%和25%。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架有效实现了时空-关系协同推理，显著提升了手术三元组识别的准确性和时间一致性，验证了模型在复杂手术场景中保持三元组因果与语义一致性的能力。

Abstract: Understanding complex surgical scenes requires recognizing multiple interdependent entities, such as instruments, actions, and targets, while maintaining their relational consistency across time. Existing surgical triplet recognition methods struggle to jointly model intra-frame label dependencies and inter-frame temporal semantics in a unified manner. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework that integrates spatial, relational, and temporal cues for robust surgical triplet recognition. Specifically, class-specific spatial priors are first extracted through a multi-scale encoder. These priors are then refined by a Label Correlation Modeling module with multi-scale class activation map-guided relational extraction (MS-CAMRE), enabling the model to capture both static co-occurrence patterns and dynamic contextual dependencies among triplet components. Furthermore, a Bidirectional Temporal-Relational Fusion Attention (BTRFA) module harmonizes temporal and relational representations to achieve coherent temporal reasoning. We also introduce a new evaluation metric, the Triplet Consistency Error Rate (TCER), which quantitatively measures the model's ability to preserve causal and semantic consistency across triplets. Extensive experiments on the CholecT45 and ProstaTD datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving AP_IVT by 5.1 percent and 7.8 percent, respectively. Moreover, according to TCER, our approach achieves relative reductions of more than 36 percent and 25 percent on the two datasets, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our framework in temporal-relational co-reasoning.

</details>


### [55] [HiSC: Hierarchical Spatial Clustering Token Compression for Efficient 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04610)
*Jiuhe Qu,Yingping Liang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiSC是一个面向3D视觉语言模型的免训练分层空间聚类令牌压缩框架，通过空间图合并和聚类剪枝两个阶段，实现了90%以上的令牌缩减且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D视觉语言模型因多视角场景中的重复观测和大量无信息区域导致令牌冗余，计算成本高昂。现有的2D令牌压缩方法无法捕捉3D场景的结构化特性，导致空间覆盖不完整和细粒度细节丢失。

Method: HiSC包含两个核心模块：(1) 空间图合并策略(SGraM)，将跨视角冗余建模为空间连通性，在LLM推理前合并物理一致的区域令牌；(2) 空间聚类剪枝范式(SCluP)，在LLM推理内部进行跨簇和簇内两级分层压缩，保留对象实例完整性同时维持重要区域的细粒度细节。

Result: 在多个3D推理基准上的实验验证了HiSC的有效性，特别是在高视觉令牌剪枝率下表现优异，可实现超过90%的令牌缩减且性能退化极小。

Conclusion: HiSC通过将令牌压缩从令牌级选择提升到聚类级处理，利用联合几何与语义线索组织空间聚类，为3D VLM提供了一种高效且有效的免训练压缩方案。

Abstract: 3D vision-language models (3D VLMs) enable spatial reasoning over multi-view scenes but suffer from substantial token redundancy due to duplicated observations and large uninformative regions, leading to high computational cost. Although visual token compression has shown promise in accelerating 2D VLMs, it fails to capture the structured nature of 3D scenes and leads to incomplete spatial coverage and loss of fine-grained details. In this paper, we propose \textbf{HiSC}, a training-free framework for hierarchical spatial clustering token compression in 3D VLMs. HiSC lifts token compression from token-level selection to cluster-level processing by organizing tokens into spatially grounded clusters using joint geometric and semantic cues. Specifically, we first introduce a \textbf{spatial graph-based merging (SGraM) strategy} that models cross-view redundancy as spatial connectivity and consolidates physically consistent regions, effectively merging extremely similar redundant tokens prior to LLM inference. We then propose a \textbf{spatial clustering-based pruning (SCluP) paradigm} within LLM inference, which performs hierarchical compression across clusters and within clusters, preserving object instance completeness while retaining fine-grained details for important regions. Extensive experiments on diverse 3D reasoning benchmarks show validate the effectiveness of HiSC, particularly under high visual token pruning ratios. Besides, HiSC achieves over 90\% token reduction with minimal performance degradation. Code is accessible at https://github.com/elecreak/HiSC.

</details>


### [56] [DAC-Pose: Dual-Agent Collaborative Framework for Pose-Guided Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04622)
*Haotian Yang,Zhile Yang,Huiyu Zhou,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAC-Pose是一个智能体驱动的多模态框架，将单视角人体生成重构为双智能体协作系统，通过先验语义推理（PSR）与差异感知视觉编码（DAVE）两个智能体的自主反馈循环，解决大视角变化下的姿态引导人体生成中的视觉伪影问题，在DeepFashion和Market-1501基准上验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统姿态引导人体生成方法在剧烈视角变化下会产生严重的视觉伪影，根本原因在于缺乏逻辑推断不可见区域和建模复杂空间形变的认知能力。

Method: 提出双智能体协作框架DAC-Pose：(1) PSR（先验语义推理）智能体作为认知引擎，通过协作推理推断不可见区域的细粒度属性；(2) DAVE（差异感知视觉编码）智能体作为视觉感知模块，量化并编码视角引起的空间错位，持续向生成过程反馈空间约束，两个智能体形成自主反馈循环。

Result: 在DeepFashion和Market-1501基准上的大量实验验证了该智能体驱动范式的优越性，DAC-Pose在剧烈视角变化下在纹理对齐和身份一致性保持方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的生成框架通过语义推理与视觉感知的协同反馈循环，能有效弥补传统方法在复杂视角变化下认知能力不足的缺陷，实现高保真细节合成。

Abstract: AI agents have emerged as a powerful new paradigm in generative image synthesis, enabling systems to perform complex semantic reasoning rather than passive pixel-level mapping. In pose-guided human generation, conventional methods inevitably produce severe visual artifacts under drastic viewpoint shifts, fundamentally because they lack the cognitive capacity to logically deduce unseen regions and model complex spatial deformations. To bridge this gap, we propose DAC-Pose, a novel agent-driven multimodal framework that reformulates single-view human generation as a collaborative dual-agent system. DAC-Pose integrates two complementary components, namely, the Prior Semantic Reasoning (PSR) agent and the Discrepancy-Aware Visual Encoding (DAVE) agent. Functioning as a cognitive engine, PSR utilizes collaborative reasoning to deduce the fine-grained attributes of unseen regions. Concurrently, acting as a specialized visual perception agent, DAVE quantifies and encodes viewpoint-induced spatial misalignments, continuously feeding robust spatial constraints back into the generative process. This autonomous feedback loop between semantic deduction and visual perception ensures high-fidelity detail synthesis. Extensive experiments on the DeepFashion and Market-1501 benchmarks validate the superiority of our agent-driven paradigm. Notably, DAC-Pose excels in preserving texture alignment and identity consistency under drastic viewpoint changes. The code is available at https://github.com/AIVRC/DAC-Pose.

</details>


### [57] [Visual Anchoring in Diffusion: Multimodal Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04623)
*Zehao Bao,Shujun Guo,Bruce X. B. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDSM-MM方法，通过"分类即生成"的扩散范式解决零样本骨架动作识别中多模态融合权重选择困难的问题，在不使用测试集自适应的情况下取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本骨架动作识别中，当未见动作类共享相似的骨架关节动态但对象或场景上下文不同时，仅靠骨架信息存在模糊性。RGB能提供缺失的线索，但现有多模态方法通常独立维护骨架和RGB评分分支再融合输出，固定融合权重无法捕捉类别对依赖的模态可靠性，自适应规则又缺乏目标侧反馈来决定哪个分支应主导。

Method: 采用"分类即生成"（classify-by-generation）范式，每个类别通过文本条件去噪器预测骨架特征中噪声的准确度来评分。该方法将逐渐损坏的骨架与固定条件分离，使RGB和文本联合条件作用于单一类别评分函数而非产生独立分数。具体实现为多模态三元组扩散用于骨架-文本匹配（TDSM-MM），在文本条件去噪Transformer中增加一个非扩散的RGB条件token作为稳定的视觉锚点。

Result: TDSM-MM在NTU-60/120四个划分中三个上取得最佳归纳准确率，并在NTU-120 96/24上超过转导式最先进方法（71.3% vs 69.1%），且无需测试时自适应。

Conclusion: 扩散式方法通过条件生成的统一架构绕过了模态融合权重选择问题，是零样本学习的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Zero-shot Skeleton Action Recognition (ZSAR) remains ambiguous when unseen actions share similar skeleton joint dynamics but differ in objects or scene context. RGB provides these missing cues, yet existing multimodal methods typically maintain independent skeleton and RGB scoring branches and fuse their outputs. Without using unlabeled test data for adaptation or fusion calibration, a fixed fusion weight cannot capture class-pair-dependent modality reliability, while an adaptive rule lacks target-side feedback for deciding which branch should dominate. We bypass this weight-selection problem via the classify-by-generation paradigm, where each class is scored by how accurately a text-conditioned denoiser predicts the noise added to the skeleton feature. This formulation separates the progressively corrupted skeleton from fixed conditioning, allowing RGB and text to jointly condition a single class-scoring function rather than produce independent scores. We instantiate this idea as Multimodal Triplet Diffusion for Skeleton-Text Matching (TDSM-MM), augmenting a text-conditioned denoising Transformer with a non-diffused RGB condition token that serves as a stable visual anchor during skeleton data reconstruction. Our proposed TDSM-MM has been ablated via extensive experiments and achieved the best inductive accuracy on three of four NTU-60/120 splits and surpasses the transductive state-of-the-art on NTU-120 96/24 (i.e., 71.3% vs. 69.1%), without test-time adaptation, suggesting that diffusion-based methods can be a promising direction for zero-shot learning.

</details>


### [58] [YOLO-PVC: 2D-to-3D Consolidation of Slice-wise Detections for Volumetric Liver Tumor Localization in MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04642)
*Talha Waqas,Mounir Lahlouh,Kawther Taibouni,Mahnoor Waqas,Salar Ahmed,Sébastien Mulé,Yasmina Leroul-Chenoune*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO-PVC，一种轻量级且与模型无关的2D-to-3D检测结果整合框架，用于将逐切片2D目标检测结果整合为连贯的3D体积预测，在3D肝脏MRI肿瘤定位任务上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐切片2D目标检测器在体数据上计算高效且易于扩展，但沿深度轴往往产生碎片化和不稳定的预测结果，需要一种结构化方法来整合并提升3D预测的连贯性与准确性。

Method: YOLO-PVC框架包含三个核心组件：深度连续性约束、基于稳健百分位统计的边界框坐标聚合，以及基于轻量级MLP的轴向范围校准模块。该框架显式处理缺失检测和异常切片问题，区别于简单的堆叠或平均策略。

Result: 在3D肝脏MRI体积数据的三类肿瘤实验中，启发式PVC实现整体IoU_3D为0.665，校准变体进一步将性能提升至0.710，同时保持较高的平面重叠度（BEV IoU约0.78），优于多种聚合基线方法。

Conclusion: 结构化几何整合方法为临床MRI中肝脏肿瘤的3D定位提供了有效且实用的解决方案，证明了在2D切片检测结果基础上进行几何整合能够显著提升体积级检测性能。

Abstract: Slice-wise 2D object detectors are increasingly applied to volumetric data due to their computational efficiency and scalability, yet they often yield fragmented and unstable predictions along the depth axis. We propose YOLO-PVC, a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for 2D-to-3D consolidation of slice-wise detections. The method enforces depth continuity, aggregates bounding box coordinates using robust percentile statistics, and further refines axial extent through a lightweight MLP-based calibration module. Unlike naïve stacking or averaging strategies, YOLO-PVC explicitly addresses missing detections and outlier slices along the depth dimension. Experiments on 3D liver MRI volumes across three tumor categories demonstrate consistent improvements over multiple aggregation baselines. The heuristic PVC achieves an overall $\mathrm{IoU}_{3D}$ of $0.665$, while the calibrated variant further improves performance to $0.710$, with high planar overlap ($\mathrm{BEV\ IoU} \approx 0.78$). These results demonstrate that structured geometric consolidation provides an effective and practical solution for volumetric liver tumor localization in clinical MRI.

</details>


### [59] [DisMix: Order-Aware Mixup for Medical Imaging via Disentangling Ordinal and Non-Ordinal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04652)
*Dileepa Pitawela,Gustavo Carneiro,Hsiang-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DisMix，一种面向序数分类的顺序感知数据增强框架。DisMix通过双码本VQ-VAE将序数特征（疾病严重程度）与非序数特征（外观变异）解耦，分别进行插值混合，从而在不破坏序数结构的前提下引入数据多样性。在四个医学影像数据集上与六种mixup基线搭配六种序数分类器进行对比，DisMix取得最佳综合性能，且在数据稀缺和临床分级变异性下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像mixup数据增强策略不适用于序数分类任务（如医学疾病分级）。由于标签编码的是疾病进展的严重程度，标准mixup不加区分地混合序数特征（严重程度线索）与非序数特征（外观变异），会扭曲支撑临床分级的关键序数结构。

Method: 引入DisMix框架：1）使用双码本VQ-VAE将图像特征解耦为序数子空间和非序数子空间；2）序数码通过插值生成有意义的中间等级；3）非序数码通过变化引入外观多样性，同时不破坏序数信号。

Result: 在四个医学影像数据集上，DisMix与六种mixup基线和六种序数分类器配对时表现最佳（综合性能最优），并且在数据稀缺和临床分级变异性场景下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: DisMix通过解耦序数与外观特征并独立混合，有效解决了标准mixup在序数分类中的结构扭曲问题，为医学影像疾病分级任务提供了一种更可靠的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Image mixup is a widely adopted data augmentation strategy, yet it is ill-suited for ordinal classification tasks such as medical disease grading, where labels encode a progression of severity. By indiscriminately blending disease-severity cues (ordinal) with appearance-level variation (non-ordinal), standard mixup produces samples that distort the very ordinal structure that underpins clinical severity grading. We introduce DisMix, an order-aware mixup framework for ordinal classification. DisMix disentangles ordinal and non-ordinal features via a dual-codebook VQ-VAE, allowing each subspace to be mixed independently: ordinal codes are interpolated to produce meaningful intermediate ranks, while non-ordinal codes are varied to introduce appearance diversity without corrupting the ordinal signal. Across four medical imaging datasets, DisMix shows the best aggregate performance among six image mixup baselines paired with six ordinal classifiers and remains effective under data scarcity and clinical grading variability.

</details>


### [60] [Overcoming Statistical Bias in Action-Controllable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04653)
*Yuhong Shi,Zhenhao Chu,Jie Wei,Jun Hao,Jianyi Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo反事实一致性框架，通过多步反事实一致性和动作-空间反事实一致性两种约束，减少动作条件世界模型对统计捷径的依赖，增强预测对动作的真实可控性。在Mini-SSMB、VP2、BAIR和RoboNet等基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型常利用视觉惯性和重复运动模式等统计捷径进行预测，导致模型的动态预测并未真正依赖于动作输入——不同动作产生相似未来，零动作下运动仍持续。如何降低模型对统计捷径的依赖是核心挑战。

Method: 提出CoCo（Counterfactual Consistency）框架，通过两种互补约束实现动作可控性：(1) 多步反事实一致性：约束参考、逆动作和零动作rollout的一致性；(2) 动作-空间反事实一致性：在镜像场景和变换动作下强制一致预测。同时提出ARC和DE指标用于评估动作可控性，以及Mini-SSMB基准用于同状态多动作反事实评估。

Result: 在Mini-SSMB上，完整模型ARC_inv达0.412、ARC_ref达0.483，DE相对基线降低17.07%；在VP2视觉规划上取得73.1%的平均成功率，超过现有SOTA模型；在BAIR和RoboNet上的实验表明方法能保持视频预测质量并跨模型设置迁移。

Conclusion: 仅注入动作特征不足以实现动作控制，必须通过反事实一致性约束来强化动作与动态之间的因果依赖。CoCo框架有效减少统计捷径依赖，提升动作可控性，且不影响预测质量，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve under an agent's actions. Yet future frames are often highly predictable from visual inertia and recurring motion patterns alone. This creates a shortcut: models can fit the data by exploiting statistical biases without making their visible dynamics meaningfully depend on the action. As a result, different actions may produce similar futures, while motion may persist even under zero action. The key question is how to reduce reliance on statistical shortcuts from dominating action-conditioned prediction. We argue that action control requires more than injecting action features; it requires enforcing consistency under counterfactual changes to actions and observations. Based on this insight, we introduce CoCo, a Counterfactual Consistency framework to enhance action controllability through two complementary constraints. Multi-step counterfactual consistency constrains reference, inverse-action, and zero-action rollouts, while action-spatial counterfactual consistency enforces consistent predictions under mirrored scenes and transformed actions. Together, they reduce reliance on statistical shortcuts from substituting for action-dependent dynamics. We further introduce Action Response Consistency (ARC) and Drift Energy (DE) to assess action controllability, together with Mini-SSMB for same-state, multi-action counterfactual evaluation. On Mini-SSMB, our full model achieved ARC_inv of 0.412 and ARC_ref of 0.483, while reducing DE by 17.07% relative to the baseline. On VP2 visual planning, it achieves the highest average success rate among SOTA models, at 73.1%. Experiments on BAIR and RoboNet further show that these gains preserve video prediction quality and transfer across model settings.

</details>


### [61] [CSGen: A Multi-Domain Curvilinear Structure Generation Model via Hierarchical Multimodal Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04655)
*Zhe Shan,Ziming Yang,Lei Zhou,Wenwen Zhang,Cong Lin,Xia Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CSGen，一种层级多模态扩散模型，用于生成与精确曲线结构对象严格对齐的高保真图像，解决了曲线结构图像可控生成这一开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线结构分析是多媒体领域的重要基础任务，但精确曲线结构对象的可控图像生成仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: CSGen包含三项关键创新：1）构建包含24K+样本、5个领域、7种标注类型的多域多模态数据集；2）提出层级渐进控制策略，通过分阶段信号注入解耦拓扑线索与视觉上下文，缓解语义漂移并保证稀疏结构的拓扑完整性；3）设计稀疏感知损失重加权机制，解决曲线结构的极端稀疏性问题，增强对细小脆弱结构的关注。

Result: 大量实验表明CSGen生成的图像具有优越的结构精度和视觉真实感，显著提升下游分割性能，并在多样提示下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CSGen被验证为一种可扩展的、以数据为中心的复杂曲线结构分析范式，适用于多种多媒体应用场景。

Abstract: Curvilinear structure analysis is an important and fundamental task in multimedia. However, the controllable generation of images with precise curvilinear structure objects remains an open challenge. To address this, we propose CSGen, a hierarchical multimodal diffusion model that synthesizes high-fidelity images precisely aligned with multiple control conditions. The CSGen is built upon three key innovations: 1) We construct a multi-domain and multimodal dataset, including over 24K samples from 5 domains and 7 different types of annotations, to train the unified generation model. 2) We propose a novel hierarchical progressive control strategy that decouples topology clues from visual context by a phased signal injection, mitigating semantic drift while ensuring the topological integrity of sparse structures. 3) We design a sparsity-aware loss re-weighting mechanism to address the extreme sparsity of curvilinear structures, significantly enhancing the attention on thin and fragile structures during optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CSGen generates images with superior structure accuracy and visual realism, significantly improving downstream segmentation performance while maintaining robustness across diverse prompts. Our results confirm CSGen as a scalable, data-centric paradigm for the analysis of complex curvilinear structures in diverse multimedia applications. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ShanZard/CSGen.

</details>


### [62] [MobileWAM: Bridging World Action Models to Mobile Manipulation with Chain-of-Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04657)
*Zehua Fan,Junjie He,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Wenqi Lyu,Linge Zhao,Fuhao Li,Zihan You,Yifei Yang,Kaiming Xu,Qi Jiang,Yue Jiang,Haoang Li,Cheng Chi,Bailin Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MobileWAM，一种基于视频生成主干的世界动作模型，通过混合Transformer架构（融合视频扩散Transformer与轻量级动作专家）实现移动操作任务的全身控制。其核心创新包括三专家混合的前馈层路由机制和Chain-of-Foresight（CoF）中间表示监督方案，在ManiSkill-HAB基准和真实ARX Lift2移动机械臂上均超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）局限于桌面操作场景，而移动操作需要同时处理全身运动和场景级动力学，但当前方法仍依赖缺乏动力学感知的视觉编码器与手工设计的协调机制。作者旨在将视频生成中的大规模运动先验迁移到移动操作任务中，弥合这一研究空白。

Method: MobileWAM采用混合Transformer架构：将预训练的视频扩散Transformer与轻量级动作专家通过逐层联合注意力融合。动作专家的每个前馈层设计为三专家混合（共享/移动/操作专家），由动作token中的运动意图软路由选择。此外提出Chain-of-Foresight（CoF）机制，让中间表示逐步预测未来潜在块序列，每个步骤基于前一步条件化，与解耦的视频-动作去噪方案配合以稠密化监督信号。

Result: MobileWAM在ManiSkill-HAB基准上超越最新的移动操作策略，并在真实ARX Lift2移动机械臂上通过微调实现多样化任务的强泛化能力。推理时仅保留策略级成本，视频生成和预测链均被丢弃，计算开销极低。

Conclusion: MobileWAM成功将视频生成驱动的世界动作模型从桌面操作扩展到移动操作领域，验证了利用互联网规模运动先验进行全身控制的可行性。CoF监督机制和解耦视频-动作去噪方案为移动操作提供了高效且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on video generation backbones are a rising recipe for robot learning, yet remain confined to tabletop manipulation. Mobile manipulation demands simultaneous locomotion and whole-body manipulation amid scene-scale dynamics, yet is still dominated by dynamics-blind visual encoders with hand-crafted coordination. We bridge this gap with MobileWAM, a mixture-of-transformers architecture that fuses a pretrained video diffusion transformer with a lightweight action expert through layerwise joint attention, translating internet-scale motion priors into whole-body control. To reconcile the heterogeneous dynamics of moving and manipulating, each feed-forward layer of the action expert becomes a three-expert mixture of shared, locomotion, and manipulation experts, softly routed by the motion intent in the action tokens. To densify supervision, we further propose Chain-of-Foresight (CoF): intermediate representations sequentially predict a chain of future latent chunks, each step conditioned on its predecessor. CoF pairs naturally with our decoupled video--action denoising scheme. At deployment, the WAM serves as a pure current-frame encoder; foresight acts only through gradients, so at inference the foresight chain and video generation are discarded, leaving only policy-level cost. MobileWAM surpasses state-of-the-art mobile manipulation policies on ManiSkill-HAB and fine-tunes to a real ARX Lift2 mobile manipulator across diverse tasks with strong generalization. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [63] [Differential 6-DOF Pose Estimation with Provable First-Order Immunity to Camera Calibration Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04673)
*Yueqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Yi Zhang,Baoqiong Wang,Wenjun Chen,Shuixin Pan,Yulan Guo,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种差分位姿估计方法，直接通过帧间图像位移和已知3D控制点恢复平台6-DOF运动，避免了传统PnP方法的绝对位姿独立估计带来的外参标定误差灵敏度问题。方法在SE(3)上建模运动，通过透视投影方程差分和深度不变近似实现，支持单目和多相机系统，并在精度、标定鲁棒性和计算效率上超越了现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-2D方法逐帧独立估计绝对相机位姿，再通过相机到平台的外参恢复平台运动，这对外参标定误差非常敏感，尤其在微运动场景下误差会被放大。作者希望提出一种直接恢复平台运动的方法，避免中间绝对位姿估计步骤，从而提升微运动估计的精度和鲁棒性。

Method: 通过差分透视投影方程，利用深度不变近似，在SE(3)上建模平台运动。方法证明平移外参误差可完全抵消，旋转外参误差仅引入有界扰动；推导了通用可观测性条件、Cramer-Rao下界和偏差消除的一致性估计器，并刻画了近似的有效性边界。支持单目和多目相机系统。

Result: 在五控制点和0.5像素图像噪声下，单目求解器获得俯仰-偏航旋转RMSE为10.09角秒、平移RMSE为3.70毫米、运行时间0.34毫秒；双目求解器旋转RMSE为10.58角秒、平移RMSE为3.91毫米、运行时间0.27毫秒。在合成和真实实验中均超越代表性PnP和广义PnP方法。

Conclusion: 差分位姿估计方法在6-DOF平台微运动估计上建立了新的SOTA，在精度、标定鲁棒性和计算效率方面均优于传统PnP方法，为微运动监测和机器人操作提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) motion estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, autonomous systems, and structural displacement monitoring. Conventional 3D-2D methods estimate absolute camera poses independently at each time and recover platform motion through camera-to-platform extrinsics, making them sensitive to extrinsic calibration errors, especially for micromotion. We present a differential pose estimation method that directly recovers platform motion from inter-frame image displacements and known 3D control points. By differencing perspective projection equations, using a depth-invariance approximation, and modeling motion on SE(3), the method avoids independent absolute-pose estimation and supports both monocular and multi-camera systems. We prove that translational extrinsic errors cancel exactly, while rotational errors induce a bounded perturbation determined by calibration error, motion magnitude, and observation geometry. We also derive generic observability conditions, a Cramer-Rao lower bound, and a bias-eliminated consistent estimator, and characterize the validity limits of the approximations. Extensive synthetic and real-world experiments establish a new state of the art for 6-DOF platform micromotion estimation, outperforming representative PnP and generalized-PnP methods in accuracy, calibration robustness, and computational efficiency. With five control points and 0.5-pixel image noise, the monocular solver obtains a combined pitch-yaw rotation RMSE of 10.09 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.70 mm, and a runtime of 0.34 ms. The binocular solver achieves a rotation RMSE of 10.58 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.91 mm, and a runtime of 0.27 ms. Code will be released upon publication at https://github.com/zyoungszu/pami2026.

</details>


### [64] [SurgNarrator: A Generative Retrieval Framework for Surgical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04676)
*Yuqing Feng,Jiawei Ma,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Kun Yuan,Nicolas Padoy,Daniel S. Elson,Anh Nguyen,Stamatia Giannarou,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgNarrator，一个面向手术视频理解的生成式检索框架。通过构建手术专属词汇表和时序感知对比学习目标，利用预训练的Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B学习判别性临床表征，并采用分层过程感知检索策略加速推理。在12个基准的零样本设置下取得一致性能提升，同时将输出延迟降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法面临两难：自回归视频-语言模型支持全面推理但延迟高，不适用于临床时间敏感场景；对比学习模型延迟低但难以处理复杂场景理解。传统生成式检索方法难以直接迁移到手术领域，因为相似视觉外观可能对应不同语义事件，且手术术语高度专科化。

Method: 1) 从手术描述中构建精心整理的手术专属词汇表，定义临床有意义的检索空间；2) 适配预训练的Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B，使用时序感知对比学习目标学习判别性临床表征；3) 推理阶段采用分层、过程感知的检索策略，将搜索空间缩小到相关手术类型，实现快速有效的响应。

Result: 在12个基准的零样本设置下进行综合评估，相比最先进的基线方法取得一致性能提升，同时相较生成式基线将输出阶段延迟降低两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: SurgNarrator验证了生成式检索范式在手术视频理解中的可行性，通过手术专属词汇构建和时序感知的学习策略有效解决了手术领域的特殊挑战，在保证推理性能的同时大幅提升了效率，使其成为临床时间敏感应用的有力候选方案。

Abstract: Surgical procedures unfold as structured and recurring clinical events, whose real-time understanding via intraoperative surgical videos is critical for intraoperative decision-making and support. However, existing video understanding methods force a trade-off: autoregressive video-language models support comprehensive reasoning but are not practical for time-sensitive clinical applications, whereas contrastive models offer low latency but struggle with complex scene understanding. Recently, generative retrieval has been explored for general-domain video understanding, but transferring it to surgery is not trivial because near-identical visual appearances may indicate semantically distinct events, and the terminology involved is highly surgery-specific. To this end, we propose SurgNarrator, a new generative retrieval framework tailored for surgical video understanding. We construct a well-curated surgery-centric vocabulary from surgical captions to define a clinically meaningful retrieval space. We then adapt the pre-trained Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B to learn discriminative clinical representations with a temporally-aware contrastive objective. During inference, a hierarchical, procedure-aware retrieval strategy narrows the search space to the relevant procedure type, delivering fast and effective responses. Our method is comprehensively evaluated on twelve benchmarks in a zero-shot setting and achieves consistent performance gains over state-of-the-art baselines, while reducing output-stage latency by more than two orders of magnitude compared with the generative baseline.

</details>


### [65] [Teaching MLLMs to Say No: Generalized Referring Expression Comprehension via Refusal Calibrated GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04698)
*Xuzheng Yang,Jun Ling,Tao Huang,Caiyan Qin,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-GRPO（拒绝校准的组相对策略优化）方法，解决广义指代表达理解（GREC）中多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）因缺乏负样本训练而产生幻觉边界框的问题，在不牺牲正样本定位精度的前提下增强模型的拒绝能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在定位存在的物体方面表现出色，但由于训练阶段缺少负样本，它们往往无法拒绝不存在的物体，产生幻觉边界框。现有的后训练方法（如SFT和RL）虽然能增强拒绝行为，但通常会降低正样本的定位精度，破坏模型的核心能力。

Method: 提出RC-GRPO，一种校准的强化学习策略：1) 在rollout中对负样本强制输出"None"以获得有效的优势估计；2) 对正样本施加惩罚以防止过度拒绝；3) 通过惩罚机制在准确性和可靠性之间取得平衡；4) 第二阶段推理强化进一步巩固因果理解和可解释性。

Result: 在三个GREC基准上的实验表明，RC-GRPO在保持强大拒绝能力的同时，取得了优越的定位精度。

Conclusion: RC-GRPO通过校准的强化学习策略有效解决了MLLMs在GREC任务中拒绝能力与定位精度之间的权衡问题，实现了两者的平衡。

Abstract: We tackle the challenging yet underexplored task of Generalized Referring Expression Comprehension (GREC), which requires a model to localize the object described by a textual expression when it exists (positive sample) and to refuse output when it does not (negative sample). Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at localizing existing objects, they often fail to reject nonexistent ones due to the absence of negative samples during training, producing hallucinated bounding boxes. Existing post-training approaches such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) enhance refusal behavior but usually degrade localization accuracy on positive samples, undermining the model's core competence. To address this, we propose Refusal-Calibrated Group Relative Policy Optimization (RC-GRPO), a calibrated RL strategy that strengthens the refusal ability of MLLMs while preserving localization performance. It enforces "None" outputs in rollouts for valid advantage estimation on negative samples and applies a penalty to prevent over-refusal on positives, achieving a balanced trade-off between accuracy and reliability. A second-stage reasoning reinforcement further consolidates causal understanding and interpretability. Experiments on three GREC benchmarks demonstrate that RC-GRPO attains superior localization accuracy while maintaining strong refusal capability.

</details>


### [66] [UniWorld-View: Large-Baseline View Synthesis via Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04701)
*Haiyang Zhou,Wangbo Yu,Chaoran Feng,Xunyu Zhou,Yonghong Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-View是一个统一的框架，用于从单目输入进行可控的大基线新视角合成。它结合显式3D引导（通过遮挡感知点云渲染获得）与视频扩散生成模型，实现精确相机控制和几何一致的视图生成，并在WorldScore基准和零样本NVS基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上大量单目视频和图像为沉浸式内容创作提供了宝贵资源，但稀疏输入下的新视角生成面临重大挑战。基于重建的方法（如NeRF和3DGS）在稀疏输入下性能严重退化且无法显式处理遮挡；生成式方法缺乏精确的几何引导，难以处理大基线视角合成。

Method: UniWorld-View将显式3D引导与生成式扩散建模相结合。几何引导通过遮挡感知的点云渲染策略获得，该策略解决了可见性歧义问题，并为扩散模型提供准确的先验。同时利用强大的视频扩散骨干网络进行合成，支持极端相机运动和宽基线变化下的新视角生成，并可为下游动态3DGS重建提供多视角视频。

Result: 在WorldScore基准和零样本新视角合成（NVS）基准上的实验表明，UniWorld-View在可控性、几何一致性和视觉保真度方面均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 通过融合显式几何引导与扩散生成，UniWorld-View成功解决了稀疏单目输入下大基线新视角合成的难题，在控制精度和几何一致性上取得了突破，并具备多视角视频生成以支持下游3D重建的能力。

Abstract: The abundance of casually captured monocular videos and images on social media provides a valuable source for immersive content creation, where generating novel views from such sparse observations can greatly enhance user experiences. However, producing photorealistic and geometrically consistent views with precise camera control remains challenging when input coverage is extremely limited. Reconstruction-based approaches such as NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) deteriorate severely under sparse inputs and fail to explicitly handle occlusions. Generative methods ease data requirements but still struggle with large-baseline view synthesis due to inaccurate or implicit geometric guidance. To overcome these limitations, we introduce UniWorld-View, a unified framework for controllable large-baseline novel view synthesis from monocular inputs. UniWorld-View integrates explicit 3D guidance with generative diffusion modeling to enable precise camera control and geometrically consistent view generation. The geometric guidance is obtained through an occlusion-aware point cloud rendering strategy that resolves visibility ambiguities and provides accurate priors for diffusion-based synthesis. By coupling this rendering strategy with powerful video diffusion backbones, UniWorld-View achieves high-fidelity novel view generation even under extreme camera motions and wide-baseline changes, and can further provide multi-view videos for downstream dynamic 3DGS reconstruction. Experiments on the WorldScore benchmark and zero-shot NVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of UniWorld-View in controllability, geometric consistency, and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [67] [A Multi-Sensor Dataset for Monitoring the Operational Environment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04704)
*Claudio Diotallevi,Rodrigo Gudiño,Zaharia Pachalieva,Philipp Neumaier,Patrick Naumann,Erik Bochinski,Volker Eiselein,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在德国数字化铁路（DSD）项目中，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH联合开发的铁路环境感知多传感器数据集，包含超过700万个高质量标注，涵盖铁路特定对象和通用感知对象，可用于AI驱动的铁路环境监测系统的训练与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化铁路系统（GoA2至GoA4）的安全高效运行依赖于可靠的环境监测，而基于AI的感知系统需要大量精确标注的数据进行训练和验证，但目前缺乏面向铁路环境感知的综合性数据集。

Method: 在Digitale Schiene Deutschland（DSD）项目框架下，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH合作开发了一个多传感器数据集，收集了不同运营场景下的数据，包含超过700万个铁路特定对象和通用感知对象的高质量标注。

Result: 数据集已完成并可供DB InfraGO AG申请使用，包含超过700万个高质量标注对象，涵盖多种运营场景，可作为推进铁路领域AI驱动环境监测的宝贵资源。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了铁路环境感知领域数据资源不足的空白，为从部分自动化（GoA2）到完全自动化（GoA4）的铁路系统AI感知技术研发提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Reliable environment monitoring is essential for the safe and efficient operation of automated railway systems, covering all Grades of Automation (GoA), from partially automated (GoA2) to fully automated operation (GoA4). Artificial Intelligence (AI) plays a central role in enabling these systems to detect, classify, and react to potential hazards in real time. The development of such AI-based perception systems requires large volumes of accurately annotated data for training and validation.
  Within the Digitale Schiene Deutschland (DSD) program, DB InfraGO AG and understandAI GmbH have developed a comprehensive multi- sensor dataset tailored to the needs of railway environment perception. This dataset contains over 7 million high-quality annotations of both railway-specific and general perception objects, captured under varying operational scenarios. The finalized dataset can now be requested at the DB InfraGO AG and serve as a valuable resource for advancing AI-driven environment monitoring in the railway domain.

</details>


### [68] [YOLOv14:Unified Cross-Domain Real-Time Object Detectionwith Adaptive Multi-View Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04720)
*Jinling Jia,Jian Lu,Jone Yawl,Chenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YOLOv14是一个统一的目标检测框架，通过四项创新（可变形区域注意力、游戏到现实域适应、多视角条件注入和自适应增强策略）来解决鱼眼畸变、游戏渲染角色、航拍视角和360°全景等非理想输入下的检测性能退化问题，在COCO val2017上达到49.1 mAP，推理速度2.91ms，并在各非理想基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时目标检测器在受控条件下表现良好，但在非理想输入（鱼眼畸变、游戏渲染角色、航拍视角、360°全景）下性能严重退化。现有方法缺乏对这些复杂场景的统一处理框架。

Method: 提出四项协同创新：(1) 可变形区域注意力（D-AAttn），用学习的2D形变场替代刚性注意力网格，实现几何畸变下的自适应采样；(2) Game2Real域适应，通过AdaIN和对抗域混淆对齐游戏与真实照片的特征分布；(3) 多视角条件注入，通过跨视角对比损失将视角嵌入注入骨干网络；(4) 自适应增强策略，自动分类输入场景类型并路由到最优增强方案，配合动态尺度路由器学习逐输入特征金字塔权重。

Result: 在COCO val2017上达到49.1 mAP，推理时间2.91ms（T4 GPU）。在非理想基准上：鱼眼+4.1 mAP，全景+6.6 mAP，无人机+6.4 mAP，游戏角色+26.1 mAP。

Conclusion: YOLOv14通过统一框架有效解决了多种非理想输入下的检测退化问题，在保持实时推理速度的同时显著提升了各非理想场景下的检测性能，尤其对游戏角色检测的提升最为显著。

Abstract: Real-time object detectors achieve remarkable accuracy under controlled conditions, yet degrade sharply on non-ideal inputs: fisheye distortion, game-rendered characters, aerial viewpoints, and 360° panoramas. We present YOLOv14, aunified detection framework addressing these challenges through four synergisticinnovations. (1) Deformable Area-Attention (D-AAttn) replaces rigid attentiongrids with learned 2D deformation fields, enabling adaptive sampling under geometric distortion. (2) Game2Real Domain Adaptation aligns rendered-game and photographic feature distributions via Adaptive Instance Normalization (AdaIN)and adversarial domain confusion, allowing game characters are detected as realhumans. (3) Multi-View Conditioning injects learned viewpoint embeddings intothe backbone with a cross-view contrastive loss that pulls same-class features fromdifferent perspectives closer. (4) An Adaptive Augmentation Policy automaticallyclassifies each input' scene type and routes to optimal augmentations, while a DynamicScaleRouter learns per-input feature pyramid weights. Together, YOLOv14achieves 49.1 mAP on COCO val2017 at 2.91 ms (T4 GPU), and delivers substantial gains on fisheye (+4.1 mAP), panorama (+6.6 mAP), drone (+6.4 mAP), andgame-character (+26.1 mAP) benchmarks

</details>


### [69] [Multi-View Face and Gesture Animation with Dynamic Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04722)
*Alireza Javanmardi,Vippin Kumar Jeetmal,Christen Millerdurai,Alain Pagani,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVFGA，一种多视角一致的3D上半身人体化身生成管线，将面部和手部分别建模并与参数化上半身网格融合，再通过3D高斯溅射实现高质量动态化身渲染，同时引入MVFGA-MoCap多视角动作捕捉数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么只关注头部而忽略手部手势，要么重建全身但难以保持面部精细保真度和手部姿态精度，无法捕捉自然交流中关键的面部表情和手部手势的细微动态。

Method: 将面部和手部分别建模，与参数化上半身网格模型融合以捕捉细粒度面部表情和手部姿态；将3D高斯溅射到所得网格上，实现从新视角高质量渲染动态化身；并构建MVFGA-MoCap多视角动作捕捉数据集。

Result: 实验表明MVFGA能生成具有高保真面部表情和手部动作的逼真化身，在上半身化身动画方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MVFGA通过面部与手部分离建模再融合的策略，有效解决了上半身化身重建中面部精细度和手部动态保真的双重挑战，为自然人类交流场景的数字人生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D human avatars with realistic upper-body motion remains challenging. Existing approaches either focus on the head and overlook hand gestures, or reconstruct the full body but fail to preserve fine-grained facial fidelity and hand pose accuracy. As a result, current methods struggle to capture the subtle dynamics of facial expressions and hand gestures that are crucial for natural human communication. While methods based on full-body parametric models enable avatar reconstruction from monocular or multi-view inputs, they often lack accurate facial animation and detailed hand articulation. To address these limitations, we propose MVFGA, a novel multi-view-consistent pipeline for generating realistic upper-body avatars. Our approach models the face and hands separately and fuses them with a parametric upper-body mesh model, enabling the capture of fine-grained facial expressions and hand poses for accurate upper-body avatar reconstruction. We then splat 3D Gaussians onto the obtained mesh, enabling high-quality rendering of dynamic avatars from novel viewpoints. Furthermore, we introduce MVFGA-MoCap, a multi-view upper-body motion capture dataset featuring controlled facial expression sequences, diverse hand gestures, and free-form communication. Experiments show that MVFGA generates visually realistic avatars with high-fidelity facial expressions and hand motions, outperforming baselines for upper-body avatar animation. Project page: https://dfki-av.github.io/MVFGA/

</details>


### [70] [Dense Metric Depth Completion from Sparse Direct Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04737)
*Hakyeong Kim,Ruicheng Wang,Chengtang Yao,Jiaolong Yang,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从稀疏dToF测量中进行稠密度量深度补全的通用框架，采用深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器和掩码联合注意力模块，在纯合成数据上训练，可零样本泛化到6个数据集和3种真实dToF设备，在精度和计算效率上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dToF传感器虽然精度高、鲁棒性强，但制造成本高、光电二极管阵列尺寸有限，导致深度图极度稀疏、低分辨率且噪声大。现有单目深度估计和深度补全方法难以处理dToF设备独特的采样模式和硬件伪影，在严重稀疏或噪声条件下性能显著下降，难以满足VR/XR、机器人等需要稠密度量深度的应用需求。

Method: 1) 提出深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器，分别处理RGB图像和稀疏dToF测量；2) 设计掩码联合注意力模块，使深度token可靠地引导图像特征而不被图像特征覆盖；3) 采用轻量级解码器高效重建稠密度量深度，无需扩散或精细后处理；4) 构建全面的dToF仿真管线，模拟flash、sub-VGA flash和旋转传感器特性，包括硬件退化、不规则稀疏性和真实噪声分布。

Result: 模型完全在合成数据上训练，在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现强零样本泛化，在精度和计算效率上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为稀疏dToF传感器的稠密度量深度补全提供了稳健实用的解决方案，具有跨传感器类型、稀疏程度和噪声条件的强泛化能力，且代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Direct Time-of-Flight (dToF) sensors provide highly accurate metric depth and are more robust than indirect ToF systems in challenging real-world conditions. However, their high manufacturing cost and limited photodiode array size produce depth maps that are extremely sparse, low-resolution, and noisy, making them unsuitable for VR/XR, robotics, and 3D perception tasks that require dense metric depth. Existing monocular and depth completion methods struggle to handle the unique sampling patterns and hardware artifacts of dToF devices, and their performance often deteriorates significantly under severe sparsity or noise. We present a generalizable framework for dense metric depth completion from sparse dToF measurements, capable of operating across diverse sensor types, sparsity levels, and noise conditions. Our model employs a depth-guided dual-branch Vision Transformer encoder that processes RGB images and sparse dToF measurements separately, while a masked joint attention module allows depth tokens to reliably guide image features without being overwritten by them. A lightweight decoder reconstructs dense metric depth efficiently, without diffusion-based or refinement-heavy post-processing. To address the scarcity of paired training data, we introduce a comprehensive dToF simulation pipeline that reproduces the characteristics of flash, sub-VGA flash, and rotating sensors, including hardware-induced degradation, irregular sparsity, and realistic noise distributions. Trained entirely on synthetic data, our model achieves strong zero-shot generalization across 6 datasets and 3 real dToF devices, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches in both accuracy and computational efficiency. This establishes a robust and practical solution for dense metric depth completion from sparse direct ToF sensors. Our code and models are open-sourced. See https://vclab.kaist.ac.kr/cvpr2026p3.

</details>


### [71] [Simile Understanding in Text-to-Image Models: An Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04750)
*Luecheng Wang,Shintaro Ozaki,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Jingun Kwon,Manabu Okumura,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的评估框架，用于研究文本到图像（t2i）模型对明喻（simile）理解的能力，通过受控数据集、YOLO目标检测自动评估指标和Diffusion Lens文本编码器层分析，揭示了模型系统性地将隐喻喻体误解为字面物体的"字面化"失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的t2i模型即使是最先进的模型，也经常在明喻提示中错误解读隐喻喻体（vehicle），将其与物体（object）混淆。这种系统性失败揭示了t2i模型在比喻语言与对象级视觉接地之间存在显著鸿沟，但目前缺乏对该问题的系统评估框架。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个组件的可扩展评估框架：(1) 受控明喻数据集——隐喻喻体从预定义的可目标检测类别中选取，与多样模板组合；(2) 基于YOLO检测的自动接地评估指标；(3) 利用Diffusion Lens进行文本编码器层分析，追踪隐喻喻体在生成过程中的涌现机制。

Result: 在多种架构不同的t2i模型上进行的实验揭示了一致的"字面化"失败模式——模型倾向于将隐喻中的喻体按字面意义理解而非按比喻意义理解。

Conclusion: 作者在讨论的基础上提出了改进t2i模型中明喻接地的潜在缓解策略，为未来提升模型对比喻语言的理解能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Similes provide a compact and expressive way to describe visual characteristics in text prompts. Recent text-to-image models (t2i models) can produce visually compelling outputs from simile prompts, yet even frontier models frequently misinterpret the metaphorical vehicle and confuse it with the object. These systematic failures reveal a gap between figurative language and object-level visual grounding in t2i models. To investigate this issue, we propose a scalable evaluation framework for simile understanding. Our framework includes (1) a controlled simile dataset in which metaphorical vehicles are drawn from a predefined set of object-detectable categories and combined with diverse templates, (2) automatic grounding metrics based on YOLO (You Only Look Once) detection, and (3) text encoder layer analysis using Diffusion Lens to track how metaphorical vehicles emerge during generation. Experiments across architecturally diverse t2i models reveal consistent literalization failure patterns. We further discuss potential mitigation strategies for improving simile grounding in t2i models.

</details>


### [72] [Revisiting Pose Sensitivity in Splat-based Computed Tomography under Sparse-view Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04752)
*Kiseok Choi,Hyeongjun Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对splat-based CT重建在真实稀疏视角条件下出现的条纹和条带伪影问题，分析发现主要源于采集几何中的位姿不准确而非视角稀疏性本身，并提出一个轻量级的梯度优化框架，在重建过程中联合精化几何参数，显著提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Splat-based断层成像（用3D高斯分布表示体积）在锥束稀疏视角CT中展现了高重建质量和快速收敛的优势，但在真实CT系统有限且非均匀的视角分布下，会出现比传统重建方法更为严重的条纹和条带伪影。作者希望通过分析伪影的真正来源并提出解决方案，使该方法能适用于实际场景。

Method: 通过详细分析证明伪影主要源于采集几何中的位姿误差而非视角稀疏性；重新审视splatting公式中的位姿敏感性，推导出一个稳定的基于梯度的框架，在重建过程中联合精化几何参数。

Result: 实验表明该公式轻量易集成，能显著提升真实稀疏视角条件下的重建保真度。

Conclusion: 研究工作不仅识别了位姿扰动如何通过可微投影算子传播，还揭示了splat-based CT对几何错位特别脆弱的原因，为实际CT系统中的应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) reconstructs volumetric representations of objects from projection images obtained by transmitting X-rays through a target. Recent splat-based tomography, which represents a volume as a continuous distribution of 3D Gaussians, has demonstrated both high reconstruction quality and fast convergence in cone-beam sparse-view CT. However, when deployed in real CT systems with limited and non-uniform view distributions, we observe distinctive streak and strip artifacts that are far more pronounced than in conventional reconstruction methods. Through detailed analysis, we show that these artifacts primarily originate from pose inaccuracies in the acquisition geometry rather than from view sparsity itself. We revisit pose sensitivity in the splatting formulation and derive a stable gradient-based framework that jointly refines geometric parameters during reconstruction. Our study not only identifies how pose perturbations propagate through the differentiable projection operator but also reveals why splat-based CT is particularly vulnerable to geometric misalignment. The resulting formulation remains lightweight and easily integrable into existing pipelines while substantially improving reconstruction fidelity under real-world sparse-view conditions.

</details>


### [73] [Trace, Verify, and Correct: A Training-Free Framework for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04759)
*Yang Yang,Jiawei Chen,Tairan Chen,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化、无需训练的框架，用于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）空间推理的验证与修正。该框架通过构建空间证据图（SEG）将CoT推理中的原子空间证据与视觉实体、空间关系等关联，并用空间证据可靠性评估（SERA）结合视觉证据判断推理是否忠实于输入图像，从而定位最早被视觉证据反驳的空间证据单元并引导模型修正后续推理。在15个模型-数据集设置下平均准确率达68.94%，比基线高8.55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在空间推理中可能产生与输入图像不一致的中间判断，错误会沿着推理链传播并影响最终答案。现有方法主要通过训练或额外空间信息来改进空间推理，但未考虑推理过程本身是否忠实于模型输入。作者发现不忠实的推理链会显著降低最终答案的准确性，因此需要一种能够验证和修正推理过程的方法。

Method: 提出一个模块化且无需训练的框架，核心包括：（1）构建空间证据图（SEG），将CoT推理中提取的原子空间证据与视觉实体、空间关系、来源步骤和视觉证据相关联；（2）空间证据可靠性评估（SERA），基于对象存在性、定位和几何测量来评估视觉证据的可靠性；（3）定位最早被可靠视觉证据反驳的空间证据单元，并引导原始MLLM修正后续推理及最终答案。

Result: 在15个模型-数据集设置上，该方法平均准确率达到68.94%，比对比基线平均高出8.55个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过验证和修正推理链中与视觉证据矛盾的空间证据，可以有效提升MLLM空间推理的准确性和忠实性。该方法无需训练即可通用地应用于不同模型和数据集，代码将开源。

Abstract: Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress, their spatial reasoning may still produce intermediate judgments inconsistent with the input image, allowing errors to propagate through the reasoning chain and affect the final answer. Existing methods mainly improve spatial reasoning through training or additional spatial information, without considering whether the reasoning process itself is faithful to the model input. Our study shows that unfaithful reasoning chains significantly reduce final-answer accuracy. To address this issue, we propose a modular and training-free framework for spatial reasoning verification and correction. The framework constructs a Spatial Evidence Graph (SEG), which associates atomic spatial evidence extracted from Chain-of-Thought reasoning with visual entities, spatial relations, source steps, and visual evidence. Spatial Evidence Reliability Assessment (SERA) evaluates the reliability of visual evidence based on object existence, localization, and geometric measurements. The framework then identifies the earliest spatial evidence unit contradicted by reliable visual evidence and guides the original MLLM to revise the subsequent reasoning and final answer. Across 15 model-dataset settings, our method achieves an average accuracy of 68.94%, outperforming the compared baselines by 8.55 percentage points on average. Our code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [74] [Splat-Based Metal Artifact Reduction in Cone-Beam CT via Compact Attenuation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04764)
*Kiseok Choi,Jaemin Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于splat的高斯表示金属伪影去除框架，将物理驱动的多色投影模型嵌入连续高斯表示中，用于锥束CT。每个高斯编码能量依赖性衰减，无需金属掩膜即可联合优化几何与材料属性，在模拟和真实数据上比现有方法收敛更快、伪影抑制更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线CT在含金属物体（如牙科填充物、骨科植入物）时会产生严重金属伪影，源于X射线的多色性打破了传统重建算法的单色假设。现有神经渲染方法虽然尝试通过可微多色投影模型解决，但仍存在平滑偏差、细结构丢失以及在大型锥束CT上计算量过大的问题。

Method: 提出一种splat-based金属伪影去除框架，将物理多色正向模型融入连续高斯表示中。每个高斯使用紧凑的材料参数化编码能量依赖性衰减，无需依赖金属掩膜即可高效联合优化几何和材料属性，从而解释金属引起的非线性效应同时保留高频结构。

Result: 在模拟和真实锥束CT扫描上的实验表明，该方法收敛速度显著更快，金属伪影抑制效果优于现有重建方法和神经场方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过紧凑的衰减公式捕获生物组织和金属植入物的关键变化，在保持高频结构的同时有效解决金属伪影问题，为大规模锥束CT金属伪影去除提供了高效且物理可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) suffers from severe metal artifacts when high-attenuation objects such as dental fillings or orthopedic implants are present. These artifacts originate from the polychromatic nature of X-rays, where attenuation varies strongly with photon energy and material composition, breaking the monochromatic assumption used by conventional reconstruction algorithms. Recent neural rendering approaches attempt to address this mismatch through differentiable polychromatic projection models, but they still struggle with smoothness bias, loss of fine structures, and prohibitive computation when extended to large-scale cone-beam CT. We introduce a splat-based metal artifact reduction framework that incorporates a physically grounded polychromatic forward model into a continuous Gaussian representation for cone-beam CT. Each Gaussian encodes the energy-dependent attenuation of the underlying material using a compact material parameterization, which enables efficient joint optimization of geometric and material properties without relying on a metal mask. This compact attenuation formulation captures the essential variation across biological tissues and metallic implants, allowing our model to explain metal-induced nonlinearity while preserving high-frequency structure. Experiments on simulated and real cone-beam CT scans show that our method converges significantly faster and suppresses metal artifacts more effectively than existing reconstruction and neural field-based approaches.

</details>


### [75] [FUSEP: A Multi-Center Benchmark for Diverse Tasks in Early Pregnancy Fetal Ultrasound Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04766)
*Bin Pu,Jiewen Yang,Liwen Wang,Ying Tan,Guannan He,Xingbo Dong,Qika Lin,Jiarong Guo,Lixian Yang,Zuozhu Liu,Shengli Li,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FUSEP，一个面向早期妊娠胎儿超声筛查的基准数据集，包含来自三家医院的4,017张超声图像和45,820个框级专家标注，覆盖14个关键解剖结构（CRL和NT两个国际指南推荐的超声切面），并报告了多种目标检测方法的基线性能，是首个公开的胎儿早期妊娠超声筛查数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球每年有大量先天性畸形新生儿出生，尤其在医疗资源欠发达地区。胎儿超声筛查是最常见的早孕解剖检测手段，但由于缺乏早期胎儿妊娠超声数据集，自动化辅助诊断的发展受到严重制约。

Method: 构建了一个包含4,017张超声图像和45,820个框级专家标注的数据集FUSEP，涵盖国际指南推荐的两个超声切面（CRL和NT），由医学专家对14个关键解剖结构进行框级标注，数据从不同超声师、设备、扫描角度和医院广泛收集，并报告了半监督学习、全监督学习、无监督域适应（UDA）和源自由UDA在超声图像多目标检测中的性能。

Result: 报告了四种学习范式（全监督、半监督、UDA、源自由UDA）在超声图像多目标检测任务上的基线性能，验证了数据集的有效性。这是首个公开可用的胎儿早期妊娠超声筛查数据集和基准。

Conclusion: FUSEP数据集和基准有望推动标准平面识别、超声图像质量控制、早期胎儿妊娠自动化辅助诊断、医学多目标检测以及目标检测域适应等多个任务的发展，为医学社区做出贡献。

Abstract: A large number of infants with congenital anomalies are born each year globally, especially in areas with underdeveloped medical resources. Currently, fetal ultrasound screening is the most common modality for early pregnancy anatomy detection. This modality can detect anomalies earlier and provide opportune treatment advice. However, the lack of an ultrasound dataset on early fetal gestation has slowed down the development of automated assisted diagnosis. In this work, we present a benchmark dataset for Fetal Ultrasound Screening in Early Pregnancy to facilitate intelligent ultrasound examination and assisted diagnosis called FUSEP. Our dataset consists of two ultrasound views recommended by the international guideline, i.e., Crown-rump Length (CRL) and Nuchal Translucency (NT) views in three hospitals, totaling 4,017 ultrasound images, with 45,820 box-level expert-level annotations. Our dataset and baseline present the following three contributions: 1) Our medical experts annotated a total of 14 key anatomical structures in two views using a box-level format; 2) Our data is collected extensively from different sonographers, devices, scanning angles, hospitals, etc; 3) We report the performance of the semi-supervised learning, fully supervised learning, unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA), and source-free UDA in ultrasound images multi-object detection. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first publicly available dataset and benchmark for fetal early pregnancy ultrasound screening. We believe that FUSEP and benchmark can contribute to the medical community in the development of multiple tasks such as standard plane recognition, quality control on ultrasound images, automated assisted diagnostics in early fetal pregnancy, medical multi-object detection, domain adaptation for object detection, etc.

</details>


### [76] [Embedding Large Language Models into Flow Controls: An Agentic Framework for Adaptive and Trustworthy Automated Cooking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04768)
*Zihan Song,Hongwei Huang,Yueshuo Sun,Yonglin Tian,Fei-Yue Wang,Bai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个智能体框架，将个性化烹饪需求系统性地分解为结构化的可验证控制程序，通过多智能体协作将用户意图转化为规范食谱、带显式流程控制的工作流程序及可执行Python代码，实现可靠的自动化烹饪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统烹饪机器人依赖预定义程序和规则控制，执行稳定但个性化能力有限；而近期的大模型方法虽支持自然语言交互，但存在决策不透明和在实际厨房中执行不可靠的问题。

Method: 提出一个由三个紧密耦合阶段组成的智能体框架：1) 离线阶段通过多智能体将食谱生成代码；2) 在线阶段通过多模态感知实现闭环执行和监管干预；3) 运行后阶段更新用户偏好模型实现长期个性化。系统将用户意图转化为规范食谱、带显式流程控制的工作流程序以及基于原子动作库的可执行Python代码。

Result: 在物理烹饪平台上进行的真实世界实验表明，该框架在多样化个性化场景中实现了可靠的任务完成、透明的执行逻辑和有效的异常处理，验证了其在真实环境中实现可信自动化烹饪的实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将语言到动作的直接映射转化为结构化的代码生成与闭环执行，兼顾了个性化需求与执行可靠性，为真实环境中可信的自动化烹饪提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Automated cooking robots have traditionally relied on predefined procedures and rule-based control, ensuring stable execution but offering limited personalization, whereas recent large-model approaches support natural language interaction but often suffer from opaque decision making and unreliable execution in real kitchens. To address this challenge, this paper proposes an agentic framework that systematically decomposes personalized cooking requirements into structured and verifiable control programs rather than directly mapping language to actions. Multiple AI agents collaboratively transform user intents into canonical recipes, workflow programs with explicit flow control, and executable Python code grounded in an atomic action library. The system consists of three tightly coupled stages: offline recipe-to-code generation through multiple agents, online closed-loop execution with supervisory intervention enabled by multimodal perception, and post-run adaptation that updates user preference models for long-term personalization. Real-world experiments on a physical cooking platform demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves reliable task completion, transparent execution logic, and effective anomaly handling across diverse personalized scenarios, validating its practicality for trustworthy automated cooking in real environments.

</details>


### [77] [On the Effectiveness of Adaptation Strategies for VLM-Based Federated Learning in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04791)
*Simon Lösche,Barış Büyüktaş,Mathis Adler,Angelos Zavras,Ioannis Papoutsis,Begüm Demir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次对联邦学习（FL）中视觉语言模型（VLM）适应策略在遥感（RS）图像分类场景下的表现进行了比较研究，评估了全微调、编码器特定微调、提示学习和低秩适应（LoRA）四种策略，从非IID数据泛化能力、通信开销和本地计算复杂度三个维度进行分析，并在BigEarthNet-S2、EuroSAT、RESISC45和ImageNet数据集上验证，最终给出了不同操作约束下VLM适应策略的选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在遥感领域面临非IID数据异构性导致的收敛困难和泛化能力下降问题。虽然VLM因其可迁移表示能力可缓解该问题，但其大参数量会显著增加通信开销和本地计算复杂度。因此需要系统研究哪些VLM适应策略能在泛化能力与通信/计算约束之间取得最佳平衡，而现有文献缺乏此类比较研究。

Method: 作者提出首个针对遥感图像分类场景下FL中VLM适应策略的比较研究框架，系统性对比四种策略：全微调（full fine-tuning）、编码器特定微调（encoder-specific fine-tuning）、提示学习（prompt learning）和低秩适应（LoRA tuning）。从三个关键标准进行评估：1）非IID数据下的泛化能力；2）通信开销；3）本地计算复杂度。实验在BigEarthNet-S2、EuroSAT、RESISC45和ImageNet四个数据集上进行。

Result: 实验揭示了任务特化、跨域泛化与效率之间存在明显的权衡关系。不同类型的VLM适应策略在不同数据集和操作约束下表现各异，不存在统一的最优策略。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，作者推导出在不同操作约束下选择合适的VLM适应策略的实用指南，可用于指导遥感图像分类任务中FL系统的设计。代码已开源公开。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training of deep learning models across decentralized image archives without requiring data centralization. This paradigm is particularly relevant in remote sensing (RS), where legal regulations, privacy concerns, and bandwidth constraints restrict data sharing. However, the presence of training data heterogeneity across clients (known as non-IID data) can impede convergence and limit the generalization capability of the aggregated global model. To mitigate the adverse effects of training data heterogeneity, vision-language models (VLMs) can be leveraged in FL due to their transferable representations, which have demonstrated robustness under distribution shifts. However, their large parameter size may substantially increase communication overhead and local computational complexity in federated settings. Therefore, it is crucial to select an appropriate VLM adaptation strategy that balances the generalization ability with the communication and computational constraints. To address this issue, in this paper, we present the first comparative study of VLM adaptation strategies for FL in the context of RS image classification. We investigate full fine-tuning, encoder-specific fine-tuning, prompt learning, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) tuning, and analyze them with respect to three criteria: 1) generalization capability under non-IID data, 2) communication overhead, and 3) local computational complexity. Experiments on BigEarthNet-S2, EuroSAT, RESISC45, and ImageNet reveal distinct trade-offs between task specialization, cross-domain generalization, and efficiency. Based on our findings, we derive a guideline for the selection of an appropriate VLM adaptation strategy in FL for RS image classification under different operational constraints. The code of this work is publicly available at https://git.tu-berlin.de/rsim/FL-RS-VLM.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluating the Diagnostic Robustness of Vision-Language Models Under Visual and Textual Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04885)
*Ali Khoramfar,Mohammad Javad Dousti,Alireza Mohamadian,Heshaam Faili*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种视觉语言模型（VLM）在脑MRI诊断任务中的鲁棒性，发现模型在保持临床证据不变的情况下，对切片顺序重排、标签位置交换和病灶移除等扰动表现出显著的预测不稳定（预测翻转率最高达67.8%），表明高准确率并不能真实反映临床可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率指标常掩盖VLM在敏感医疗领域中的可靠性缺陷，现有评估忽视了对输入呈现顺序和文本框架的敏感性，而这些问题不会被聚合准确率捕获。

Method: 使用经组织病理学验证的脑MRI数据集，对四种VLM家族进行系统评估。采用三种证据保持扰动策略：（1）重排列解剖切片顺序；（2）交换目标标签位置；（3）移除专家标注的病灶切片作为阴性对照。

Result: 序列反转导致最高48.9%的预测翻转；标签重排引发最高67.8%的不一致诊断；阴性对照显示最高76.1%的案例在病灶切片被移除后仍生成分类诊断。

Conclusion: 高准确率会高估临床可靠性，需要引入基于稳定性的评估指标来确保VLM在安全关键型临床应用中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Standard accuracy metrics for VLMs often mask significant reliability failures in sensitive domains. In this work, we utilize a histopathology-validated brain MRI dataset to systematically assess the diagnostic robustness of four VLM families under evidence-preserving perturbations. By reordering anatomical slices and swapping target label positions, we evaluate whether models maintain consistent predictions when clinical evidence remains invariant. Our results reveal significant vulnerabilities in presentation-order stability, with models exhibiting prediction flips in up to 48.9% of cases under simple sequence reversals. We further identify a textual selection bias, where label reordering triggers inconsistent diagnoses in up to 67.8% of cases despite identical visual inputs. Negative-control tests further reveal diagnostic overcommitment: models generate categorical diagnoses in up to 76.1% of cases after expert-annotated lesion slices are removed. These results demonstrate that high accuracy can overestimate clinical reliability, masking sensitivity to sequential presentation and textual framing that is not captured by aggregate accuracy. Our findings highlight the necessity of stability-based metrics for the deployment of VLMs in safety-critical clinical applications. Our evaluation data and code will be made public upon acceptance.

</details>


### [79] [Segmentation Pre-training for Label-Efficient Lumbar Spine Degeneration Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04810)
*Monzon Maria,Zisserman Andrew,Jutzeler Catherine R.,Jamaludin Amir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在腰椎MRI退行性病变分级任务中，利用自动分割伪标签进行预训练能否替代部分昂贵的人工分级标注。在约2000名受试者、11种病理的多中心数据集上，3D ResNet预训练（Dice=0.94）在所有标注比例下均提升宏平均ROC-AUC，仅需20%的分级标签即可接近全监督性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰椎MRI退行性病变的自动分级需要大规模专家标注数据，获取成本极高；而分割伪标签可通过自动化工具以近乎零的放射科医生成本生成。作者希望探索用廉价的分割预训练替代部分昂贵的人工分级标注，降低下游监督学习的标注负担。

Method: 首先使用3D ResNet编码器在椎骨、椎间盘和椎管的分割任务上进行预训练（以伪标签为监督，Dice=0.94）；随后在下游分级任务中，冻结或微调编码器并训练轻量级任务特定的分级头，使用训练数据的10%到100%不同比例进行微调，评估分割预训练对各标注比例的增益效果。

Result: 在多中心约2000名受试者、11种病理的数据集上，分割预训练在所有标注比例下均提升了任务平均（宏）one-vs-rest ROC-AUC。仅使用20%的分级标签即可达到接近全监督的性能水平，对低患病率或空间定位明确的病理获益最大。

Conclusion: 分割预训练可以有效地替代相当一部分人工分级标注需求，显著降低腰椎MRI退行性病变自动评估的标注成本，尤其在标注资源有限的实际临床场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Automated assessment of degenerative pathology in the lumbar spine on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) requires access to large-scale datasets of expert-annotated radiological gradings. In contrast, segmentation pseudo-labels can be generated by automated tools at negligible radiologist cost. We examine whether pre-training on segmentation can effectively replace a fraction of the manual grading annotations required for downstream supervision. We pre-train a 3D ResNet encoder to segment the vertebrae, intervertebral discs (IVDs), and the spinal canal, then fine-tune lightweight task-specific grading heads using different proportions of the available training data, ranging from $10\%$ to $100\%$. On a multicentre dataset of ${\sim}2{,}000$ subjects across 11 pathologies, segmentation pre-training, achieving a Dice score of $0.94$ against pseudo-labels, improved the task-averaged (macro) one-vs-rest ROC-AUC at all proportions. With only 20\% of grading labels after pre-training, the method achieved near full-supervision performance, with the largest gains observed for either low-prevalence or spatially grounded pathologies.

</details>


### [80] [UG-UMRE: Uncertainty-Guided Modality Augmentation and Distributional Calibration for Unified Multimodal Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04949)
*Bo Kong,Liruiz Jia,Yi Liang,Chao Liu,Dongfang Han,Tianwei Yan,Yuan Liu,Shengquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UG-UMRE网络，通过不确定性驱动的单模态增强模块（UDUA）和联合偶然不确定性对齐模块（JAUA）分别解决统一多模态关系抽取中的噪声传播和模态分布异构问题，在三个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态关系抽取（UMRE）研究存在两个关键问题：一是忽略固有偶然不确定性导致噪声传播；二是不同模态分布之间的深层异构性阻碍跨模态对齐。

Method: 提出UG-UMRE网络，包含两个核心模块：(1) UDUA模块，基于变分信息瓶颈将特征建模为高斯分布，结合不确定性感知的自监督对比学习机制，在保持语义一致性的同时过滤噪声；(2) JAUA模块，作为全局语义预校准机制，利用概率分布一致性构建共享潜在空间，通过同步跨模态统计属性消除分布差异。

Result: 在UMRE、MORE和MNRE三个基准数据集上进行实验，UG-UMRE取得了最先进的性能；进一步的分析验证了UDUA和JAUA模块的可插拔性和有效性。

Conclusion: UG-UMRE通过不确定性引导的方式有效解决了多模态关系抽取中的噪声传播和模态对齐问题，两个模块具有良好的模块化和可扩展性。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Relation Extraction (UMRE) aims to identify intra-modal and cross-modal relations between textual entities and visual objects. However, existing UMRE studies still encounter two critical issues: ignoring inherent aleatoric uncertainty causes noise propagation, and deep-seated heterogeneity between distinct modal distributions hinders alignment. To address these issues, we propose the Uncertainty-Guided UMRE Network (UG-UMRE). Specifically, we design an Uncertainty-Driven Unimodal Augmentation (UDUA) module, which models features as Gaussian distributions based on the Variational Information Bottleneck. By incorporating an uncertainty-aware self-supervised contrastive learning mechanism, UDUA effectively filters out noise while maintaining semantic consistency. Furthermore, we introduce the Joint Aleatoric Uncertainty Alignment (JAUA) module as a global semantic pre-calibration mechanism. JAUA leverages probabilistic distribution consistency to construct a shared latent space, eliminating the distributional gap by synchronizing cross-modal statistical properties, thereby laying a robust foundation for fine-grained interaction. Experiments on three benchmark datasets (UMRE, MORE, and MNRE) demonstrate that UG-UMRE achieves state-of-the-art performance. Further analysis validates the pluggable and effective performance of the proposed UDUA and JAUA modules.

</details>


### [81] [StaticSegFormer: An Efficient High-Performance Semantic Segmentation Based on Static Structured Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04811)
*Timo Bartels,Danish Nazir,Jan Piewek,Thorsten Bagdonat,Tim Fingscheidt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StaticSegFormer，一种针对SegFormer网络注意力层的静态结构化剪枝方法，在保持mIoU不下降的同时，相比动态剪枝方法在GPU上实现了更高帧率和更低FLOPs，在Cityscapes数据集上帧率提升高达34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态结构化剪枝方法虽然能在FLOPs和mIoU上与静态方法相当，但在GPU平台上帧率极低，远不如简单的静态方法。作者旨在解决这一效率瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出一种针对注意力层的静态结构化剪枝方法（StaticSegFormer），通过静态剪枝注意力层参数，在不牺牲mIoU的前提下，同时降低FLOPs并提高帧率。

Result: 在Cityscapes数据集上帧率相对提升高达34%，且mIoU完全没有下降；在ADE20K和Cityscapes基准上验证了方法有效性；在编码器较小和大图像场景下效果最强。

Conclusion: 静态结构化剪枝是比动态剪枝更实用的选择，尤其在实际GPU推理场景中，StaticSegFormer能够同时兼顾效率与精度。

Abstract: Structured pruning enhances the efficiency of deep neural networks (DNNs) by eliminating groups of parameters during inference. Previous methods mostly reduce computational complexity (FLOPs), while semantic segmentation performance (mIoU) slightly drops. Accordingly, recent dynamic structured pruning methods aim at reducing the performance drop, while lowering the FLOPs even more. However, on the ADE20K and Cityscapes benchmarks, our study reveals that on a GPU platform such dynamic methods exhibit a surprisingly low frame rate far below a simple static approach, while having comparable results in mIoU and FLOPs. To address this issue, we propose a static structured pruning method for attention layers, that achieves both, a lower FLOPs and a high frame rate [fps] of the SegFormer network, the latter increased by up to 34% relative on the Cityscapes dataset, while having no mIoU performance drop at all. Our so-called StaticSegFormer method is strongest for small encoders and large images.

</details>


### [82] [Rethinking Pixel Mean Flows via Interval Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04818)
*Alexander Zaytsev,Dmitry Baranchuk,Alexander Korotin,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Interval Denoiser，一种理论上严谨的潜在自由生成框架，直接从流匹配ODE推导出中间轨迹状态的精确解析映射，在ImageNet 256x256上仅用1步（FID 4.55）和2步（FID 3.98）即达到优秀性能，且无需感知损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代扩散和流模型正逐步转向少步、潜在自由的生成方式，以避免多步采样的计算开销和外部自编码器的重建瓶颈。然而，现有公式在中间轨迹状态的处理上存在理论缺陷，难以在像素级别上进行有效回归。

Method: 从流匹配ODE直接推导出中间轨迹状态的精确解析映射；证明预测位于低维流形上从而使网络直接在像素上回归变得可行；通过避免经验性的代数替换，正确隔离纯时间导数以保证无偏梯度评估和精确一阶优化；引入残差裁剪和时间采样课程，实现长间隔有效训练。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上从零训练，1步（1-NFE）达到FID 4.55，2步（2-NFE）达到FID 3.98，且不使用感知损失。

Conclusion: Interval Denoiser通过严格的理论推导为潜在自由、少步生成提供了可靠框架，在无感知损失的情况下达到了极具竞争力的少步生成性能。

Abstract: Modern diffusion and flow-based models are increasingly moving toward few-step, latent-free generation to bypass the computational overhead of multi-step sampling and the reconstruction bottlenecks of external autoencoders. We propose the Interval Denoiser, a theoretically rigorous framework for latent-free generation. Derived directly from the flow matching ODE, it establishes an exact analytical mapping for intermediate trajectory states. Unlike prior formulations, our prediction is shown to reside on a low-dimensional manifold across any time interval, making the regression tractable for a network operating directly on pixels. Furthermore, by avoiding empirical algebraic substitutions, our formulation correctly isolates the pure time derivative to prevent biased gradient evaluations and ensure exact first-order optimization. By analyzing this objective, we equip our framework with residual clipping and a time-sampling curriculum, enabling effective long-interval training and improving few-step performance. Trained from scratch on ImageNet 256x256, our model achieves an FID of 4.55 in one step (1-NFE) and 3.98 in two steps (2-NFE) without perceptual losses.

</details>


### [83] [When Diffusion Models Forget Who You Are: Identity Preservation in Face Inpainting under Large Occlusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04820)
*Feng Ding,Shuhuai Xie,Yue Zhou,Yulan Zhang,Guopu Zhu,Mengyao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReSem-Face，一个级联扩散框架，通过显式的身份条件语义先验实现多参考人脸修复，在严重遮挡和文本引导冲突下保持身份保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的人脸修复方法在严重遮挡和文本引导冲突的情况下难以保持身份保真度。
