Method: 从多个参考图像中提取代表性身份特征以重建缺失的语义区域，并通过多流条件架构引导扩散过程，提供强语义约束并兼容提示驱动的编辑。

Result: 在CelebAHQ-IDI-5和VGGFace2数据集上，ReSem-Face在严重语义掩码下实现了更可靠的身份保持修复，并相比代表性基线方法提升了文本控制的编辑质量。

Conclusion: 显式的身份条件语义先验结合多流条件架构能够有效解决扩散模型在人脸修复中身份保真度不足的问题。

Abstract: Face inpainting with diffusion models has recently achieved impressive visual quality, yet preserving identity fidelity under significant occlusion and conflicting text guidance remains a major challenge. To address this issue, we present Reference Semantic Inpainting for Face (ReSem-Face), a cascaded diffusion framework that introduces an explicit identity-conditioned semantic prior for multi-reference face inpainting. Our approach distills representative identity features from multiple references to reconstruct missing semantic regions, which then guide the diffusion process through a multi-stream conditioning architecture. This design provides strong semantic constraints when pixels are absent and stabilizes identity reconstruction while remaining compatible with prompt-driven edits. Experiments on CelebAHQ-IDI-5 and VGGFace2 demonstrate that ReSem-Face yields more reliable identity-preserving completion under severe semantic masks and improves text-controlled editing quality compared with representative baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Global Attention-Fused Image Cropping with Attention-Guided and Global-Aligned Crop Evaluator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04821)
*Haotian Yang,Zhile Yang,Kin-Man Lam,Patrick Le Callet,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全局注意力融合图像裁剪方法（GAFIC），通过注意力引导的特征融合和全局对齐的裁剪评估器，在裁剪时同时考虑局部细节与全局结构关系，在GAIC和CPC数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像裁剪方法主要关注显著区域，对图像主要组成部分之间的全局关系敏感度有限，导致裁剪结果在审美上不够理想。

Method: 提出GAFIC框架，包含两个核心模块：注意力引导特征融合（AGFF）聚合局部区域重要性构建全局表征，全局对齐裁剪评估器（GACE）将候选裁剪特征与全局表征对齐以保持边界变化敏感性；同时结合多尺度三种排序损失获取准确稳定的裁剪分数。

Result: 在GAIC和CPC数据集上的大量实验表明，GAFIC在准确性和稳定性方面均优于现有图像裁剪方法。

Conclusion: GAFIC通过选择而非合成的方式保留像素完整性，适合需要像素保真和高效批量处理的场景，区别于接缝裁剪、修复和基于扩散的合成方法。

Abstract: Image cropping aims to improve image aesthetics by preserving important content within an appropriately composed region. However, most existing methods focus primarily on salient regions and therefore have limited sensitivity to the global relationships among the main image components. To address this limitation, we propose Global Attention-Fused Image Cropping (GAFIC), which consists of an Attention-Guided Feature Fusion (AGFF) and a Global-Aligned Crop Evaluator (GACE). AGFF aggregates the importance of local regions to construct a global representation that captures both image structure and local details. GACE aligns candidate crop features with this global representation, enabling crop evaluation to remain sensitive to boundary changes. We further combine three ranking losses across multiple scales to obtain accurate and stable crop scores. Extensive experiments on the GAIC and CPC datasets demonstrate that GAFIC outperforms existing image-cropping methods, particularly in terms of accuracy and stability. Unlike pixel-level retargeting methods such as seam carving, inpainting, and diffusion-based synthesis, GAFIC does not synthesize or modify the retained pixels; instead, it selects an aesthetically preferred crop from the source image, making it suitable for scenarios where pixel integrity and efficient batch processing are important. The source code is available at https://github.com/AIVRC/GAFIC.git.

</details>


### [85] [RegisterBridgeMM: A Register-Centric Framework for RGB-Infrared Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04833)
*Zian Wang,Hangchuan Liang,Yuehua Chen,Changchun Li,Chaoyi Guo,Mingzhe Liu,Fangming Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RegisterBridgeMM，一种基于register媒介的RGB-IR图像融合检测框架，通过三阶段register生命周期（聚合、桥接、投影）实现跨模态通信，在四个基准数据集上以冻结主干网络取得了最优的mAP50-95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RGB-IR融合方法常以受限的适应机制换取轻量化的patch级交互，导致表达能力受限；同时作者观察到预训练register token同时包含模态共享与模态特有信息，可作为跨模态通信的紧凑载体。

Method: 提出三阶段register生命周期框架：1) Aggregate阶段保留预训练中每模态的register摘要；2) Bridge阶段执行双向register-to-patch读取，并带有共识残差调节；3) Project阶段将register摘要转化为空间自适应校准，用于调整patch特征。该路径避免了密集的patch-to-patch跨模态交互，同时保持预训练patch表示。

Result: 在两个骨干网络流均冻结的情况下，RegisterBridgeMM在LLVIP、M3FD、DroneVehicle和FLIR-Aligned四个基准数据集上的mAP50-95均优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: RegisterBridgeMM验证了利用预训练register token作为跨模态通信紧凑基质的有效性，在无需密集patch交互且保持预训练表示的情况下实现了最优的RGB-IR检测性能。

Abstract: RGB-infrared (RGB-IR) object detection benefits from complementary visible and thermal cues, but effective fusion remains challenging under illumination changes, weather variation, and cluttered scenes. Existing RGB-IR fusion methods often trade expressive patch-level interaction for lighter but more constrained adaptation mechanisms. We empirically observe that pretrained register tokens contain both modality-shared and modality-specific information on paired RGB-IR inputs, suggesting that they can serve as a compact substrate for cross-modal communication. Building on this observation, we propose RegisterBridgeMM, a register-mediated fusion framework organized as a three-stage register lifecycle. Aggregate preserves per-modality register summarization inherited from pretraining; Bridge performs bidirectional register-to-patch reading with consensus-residual regulation; and Project translates the resulting register summary into spatially adaptive calibration of patch features. This register pathway avoids dense patch-to-patch cross-modal interaction while preserving the pretrained patch representation. With both backbone streams frozen, RegisterBridgeMM achieves the highest mAP50-95 among the evaluated methods on all four benchmarks: LLVIP, M3FD, DroneVehicle, and FLIR-Aligned.

</details>


### [86] [Towards a satellite image manipulation and deepfake localization benchmark dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04840)
*Jacob Arndt,Debvrat Varshney,Philipe Dias,Nivedita Nukavarapu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于卫星图像篡改检测与定位的原型基准数据集，包含30张经过三种方式篡改的图像和30张真实图像，每张图像配有ground-truth掩码和采集元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的发展，伪造卫星图像（deepfakes）对遥感领域构成严重威胁，但现有数据集缺乏高质量、细粒度的篡改操作标注，无法有效训练和评估检测与定位算法。

Method: 构建了一个包含60张图像的原型数据集（30张篡改+30张真实），采用三种篡改方式（复制粘贴拼接和扩散模型修复），每张图像附带ground-truth掩码和采集元数据，支持像素级定位评估。

Result: 发布了一个可下载的原型数据集（fmow-fake-small），支持篡改检测、像素级定位、元数据研究和采集参数与检测性能关系的分析。

Conclusion: 该原型数据集填补了遥感领域高质量细粒度篡改数据的空白，为地理空间deepfake检测和图像取证研究提供了初步基准。

Abstract: Verifying the authenticity of satellite imagery has become increasingly critical given advances in generative artificial intelligence. Highly realistic synthetic imagery produced for malicious purposes (deepfakes) can have major consequences in the remote sensing domain, where this data is a fundamental source of information for science applications, planning, logistics, and monitoring. The remote sensing community lacks high-quality, fine-grained manipulation datasets suitable for training and evaluating detection and image forensics algorithms. Existing datasets are lacking and those that do exist either provide no ground truth masks for evaluating manipulation localization, or consist of entire images generated by GANs or diffusion models, which are inadequate for measuring localization performance. To address this gap, we describe a preliminary dataset construction process and prototype benchmark dataset for satellite image manipulation detection and localization. The dataset contains 60 images total, with 30 images carefully manipulated using three manipulation types including copy-paste splicing and diffusion model inpainting, and 30 authentic images. Each image is accompanied by a ground-truth mask and acquisition metadata, enabling both pixel-level localization metrics, image metadata studies, and analyses of how manipulation detection performance relates to image collection parameters. We describe the dataset construction process and present this initial release to support further research in image forensics and geospatial deepfake detection. The prototype dataset can be downloaded at https://huggingface.co/datasets/geodf/fmow-fake-small.

</details>


### [87] [Cooking beyond Frames: A Stereo Event Camera Dataset in the Kitchen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04865)
*Chengming Feng,Hesam Araghi,Liming Zheng,Julien Dupeyroux,Xucong Zhang,Jan van Gemert,Nergis Tömen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EventKitchen，一个大规模立体事件相机基准数据集，记录10名参与者在13个不同厨房中自然进行烹饪活动（无脚本动作）的5.5小时立体事件数据，并提供了10,762个动作片段和13,482个边界框的人类标注，用于支持动作识别、目标检测和立体深度估计等事件视觉任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机研究多集中于自动驾驶和无人机领域，人类日常场景的数据集严重不足；且已有的少数事件基人类活动数据集通常使用脚本化动作录制，无法捕捉真实自然的人类行为。

Method: 通过让10名参与者佩戴装有多传感器的头盔，在13个不同厨房中自然进行烹饪活动（无任何脚本动作），以自我中心视角采集5.5小时的立体事件相机数据，同时同步记录RGB、深度和IMU数据，并人工标注动作片段和边界框。

Result: EventKitchen包含5.5小时立体事件记录、10,762个动作片段标注和13,482个边界框标注；在该数据集上训练基线模型执行动作识别、目标检测和立体深度估计任务，建立了超越自动驾驶领域的事件视觉挑战基准。

Conclusion: EventKitchen通过捕捉自然真实的人类活动，填补了事件视觉领域中人本日常场景数据集的空白，为超越自动驾驶的事件视觉研究提供了具有挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Event cameras, also known as neuromorphic cameras, have gained significant attention in recent years due to their high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and low power consumption. While many studies and datasets in neuromorphic vision have focused on automotive and drone applications, human-centric daily-life scenarios remain largely underrepresented, despite their importance for developing and benchmarking event-based perception systems. Moreover, the few existing event-based human activity datasets are typically recorded with scripted human actions, limiting their ability to capture natural human behaviors. In this paper, we introduce EventKitchen, a large-scale stereo event camera benchmark dataset of human cooking activities in the kitchen. EventKitchen is egocentrically collected from 10 participants in 13 diverse kitchens, where the participants wear a helmet with multiple sensors and naturally perform cooking activities, without any scripted actions. EventKitchen comprises 5.5 hours of stereo event recordings with synchronized RGB, depth, and IMU data. We provide human annotations for 10,762 action segments and 13,482 bounding boxes. We train baseline models on EventKitchen to perform multiple event-based tasks, including action recognition, object detection, and stereo depth estimation. By capturing natural, real-world human activities, EventKitchen establishes a challenging benchmark for neuromorphic vision beyond autonomous driving.

</details>


### [88] [Persistent Object Narratives for Token-Efficient Video Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04866)
*Junzhe Chen,Siyuan Meng,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotNarrative，一种基于槽位的视频视觉接口，将视频组织为持续的对象叙事，仅用144个视觉token位置即可为冻结的Video-LLM提供紧凑、结构化且具有时间组织的视觉表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLM的视觉接口token密集且缺乏显式结构来关联跨时间的重复对象证据，导致效率低且难以捕捉长期对象关系。

Method: 首先将视觉特征分组为对象槽（object-like slots），然后通过轻量级、无参数的记忆机制整合多种互补匹配线索，将重复观察与片段级对象条目关联。每个保留条目被序列化为两种token类型：身份token（总结持续对象外观）和状态token（编码片段级外观、几何、可见性和轨迹信息）。

Result: 该方法对冻结的Video-LLM仅分配144个视觉token位置，且不依赖采样帧数。在多个数据集上，相比先前紧凑型Video-LLM接口，在准确性和视觉token数量之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 持续对象叙事被证明是一种紧凑、结构化且具有时间组织的Video-LLM视觉接口，代码将公开。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) have made strong progress in open-ended video understanding. However, their visual interfaces remain token-intensive and provide limited explicit structure for linking recurring object evidence across time. We introduce SlotNarrative, a slot-based interface that organizes a video into persistent object narratives represented by compact object-state tokens. Rather than compressing frame-wise features before establishing temporal correspondence, SlotNarrative first groups visual features into object-like slots and then associates recurring observations with clip-level object entries through a lightweight, parameter-free memory that integrates multiple complementary matching cues. Each retained entry is serialized into two token types: an identity token that summarizes persistent object appearance and a set of state tokens that encode segment-level appearance, geometry, visibility, and trajectory information. This design yields an interface of only 144 allocated visual-token positions for a frozen Video-LLM, independent of the number of sampled frames. Across multiple datasets, SlotNarrative achieves a favorable trade-off between accuracy and visual-token count compared with prior compact Video-LLM interfaces. Experimental results establish persistent object narratives as a compact, structured, and temporally organized visual interface for Video-LLMs. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [STEP-OPD: Rethinking Output Targets and Internal Dynamics in On-Policy Distillation for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04887)
*Qingyan Wei,Guangzhao Li,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Xiaohong Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP-OPD，一种面向图像生成的在线蒸馏框架。它超越传统输出级监督，通过扩展学习目标（在教师速度基础上叠加任务教师与共享基础模型间的速度差）和对齐学生与教师的分块表示变化，使蒸馏后的统一学生模型在组合对齐、文本渲染和人类偏好等任务上超越单任务教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线蒸馏（OPD）方法仅将学生优化到与教师输出速度匹配，导致教师成为优化目标的上限。同时缺乏对内部块级表示演化的约束，削弱了跨层逐步发展能力迁移的效果，限制了学生超越教师的能力。

Method: STEP-OPD包含两个核心设计：(1) 输出外推——利用任务特定教师与共享基础模型之间的速度差异作为进一步学习方向，将缩放后的该差异叠加到教师速度上，使学习目标超越教师本身；(2) 表示变化对齐——在学生与教师之间对齐块级表示变化的方向和幅度，引导学生学习网络各块间表示的逐步变换过程。

Result: 在组合对齐、文本渲染和人类偏好实验中，STEP-OPD一致优于标准OPD方法。具体而言，将DiffusionOPD的GenEval分数从0.927提升至0.961，同时改善了OCR和所有基于偏好的指标。统一学生模型在三大能力组上均超过了对应的单任务教师。

Conclusion: 输出外推能够实现超越教师的学习，表示变化对齐为学生内部表示变换提供了互补的引导。两者的结合使统一学生模型不仅超越现有OPD方法，还能在各项能力上胜过单任务教师，验证了该框架的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become an effective approach for consolidating multiple task-specialized image generation models into a single student. However, existing OPD methods optimize the student mainly to match the teacher's output velocity, making the teacher the upper limit of the optimization objective. While output-level supervision alone leaves the student's blockwise representation evolution underconstrained, which weakens the transfer of capabilities that must be progressively developed across layers. We propose STEP-OPD, an on-policy distillation framework for image generation that extends the student's learning target beyond the teacher and introduces explicit constraints on its internal representation evolution. Instead of treating the teacher as the final target, we use the velocity difference between each task-specific teacher and the shared base model as a direction for further learning and add a scaled version of this difference to the teacher velocity. In addition, we align the direction and magnitude of representation changes between the student and teacher, enabling the student to learn how representations are progressively transformed across network blocks. Experiments on compositional alignment, text rendering, and human preference show that our method consistently improves Standard OPD methods. In particular, it increases the GenEval score of DiffusionOPD from 0.927 to 0.961, while also improving OCR and all preference-based metrics. The resulting unified student surpasses the corresponding single-task teachers across all three capability groups, showing that output extrapolation enables beyond-teacher learning. And representation change alignment provides complementary guidance for the student's internal transformations.

</details>


### [90] [Visual Representation Matters: Exploiting Temporal Differences in Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04902)
*Zehua Chen,Junyou Wang,Yuxuan Jiang,Zhenying Fang,Yusheng Dai,Jianfei Chen,Ziwei Liu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TD-V2A，利用时序差分（TD）作为关键视觉表示来增强视频到音频生成，通过层级连续学习策略和退火时序差分引导方法，以最小架构改动提升V2A生成质量，在基准数据集上超越了对比视听预训练等专用V2A表示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于条件扩散的V2A方法通常需要额外的音频-视觉监督、声学结构预测或大型多模态模型推理，引入了额外网络或强归纳偏置，增加了系统复杂性和训练难度。作者希望找到一种更简洁的方式来区分V2A与I2A，利用视频中特有的时序信息增强视觉条件，同时避免复杂的外部模块。

Method: 1) 在帧级和特征级分别探究时序差分（TD）表示，确定TD在最有效的表示层级上补充视觉表征；2) 基于研究发现，提出层级连续学习策略，在扩散训练过程中逐步学习TD信息；3) 提出退火时序差分引导方法，在采样过程中逐步利用TD信息进行引导。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，有效利用TD信息能显著提升端到端V2A生成质量，甚至超越了对比音频-视觉预训练（CLAP）等专用V2A表示方法的性能。

Conclusion: 时序差分是区分V2A与I2A的关键视觉表征，通过最小化的架构修改和有效的训练/采样策略，即可实现优于复杂专用V2A表示方法的生成质量，为V2A生成领域提供了一种简洁高效的解决思路。

Abstract: Video-to-audio (V2A) generation extends image-to-audio generation (I2A) by introducing consecutive frames that provide essential temporal cues for audio synthesis. However, existing conditional diffusion-based V2A methods typically enhance visual conditioning with additional audio-visual supervision, acoustic structure prediction, or reasoning from large multimodal models, requiring extra networks or strong inductive biases. Inspired by recent advances in visual representation learning, we introduce TD-V2A, which leverages temporal differences (TD) as the key representation that distinguishes V2A from I2A, enriching visual conditioning with minimal architectural modification. We first investigate TD at both the frame and feature levels to identify the most effective representation level at which TD complements visual representations. Based on these findings, we develop a hierarchically continual learning strategy and an annealed temporal differences guidance method to progressively learn and exploit TD information during diffusion training and sampling process, respectively. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that effectively exploiting TD through our proposed framework significantly improves end-to-end V2A generation quality, even outperforming dedicated V2A representations such as contrastive audio-visual pretraining.

</details>


### [91] [Enhancing Low Back Pain Assessment with Diffusion Models for Lumbar Spine MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04906)
*Maria Monzon,Thomas Iff,Ender Konukoglu,Catherine R. Jutzeler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于扩散模型的框架SpineSegDiff，用于腰椎MRI的语义分割，不区分T1或T2加权图像，性能与最先进的nnUnet相当，尤其在退化椎间盘识别上有优势，同时生成的不确定性图为临床审查提供了有价值的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰椎间盘突出等退行性病变导致的腰背痛（LBP）是常见临床问题，腰椎MRI的精准分割对诊断至关重要。现有方法在不同加权序列（T1/T2）下的鲁棒性和对退化椎间盘的识别能力有限，且缺乏不确定性评估，难以支撑可靠临床决策。

Method: 提出了一个基于扩散模型的分割框架SpineSegDiff，在SPIDER数据集上对椎体、椎间盘和椎管进行语义分割，并与包括nnUnet在内的先进非扩散模型进行对比。该框架能够同时处理T1和T2加权MRI图像，并生成不确定性图辅助临床检查。

Result: SpineSegDiff的分割性能达到了与最先进的非扩散nnUnet相当的水平，特别是在退化椎间盘的识别上表现更优。此外，模型生成的不确定性图为临床审查提供了有价值的洞察，增强了分割结果的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了扩散模型在增强腰背痛病理脊柱MRI分析方面的潜力，有望通过更精确的分割提升腰椎退行性疾病的诊断和管理水平。

Abstract: This study introduces a diffusion-based framework for robust and accurate semantic segmentation of lumbar spine MRI scans from patients with low back pain (LBP), regardless of whether the scans are T1- or T2-weighted. We compared with advanced models for segmenting vertebrae, intervertebral discs (IVDs), and spinal canal using the SPIDER dataset. The results showed that SpineSegDiff achieved a segmentation performance comparable to that of the state-of-the-art non-diffusion nnUnet, particularly in improving the identification of degenerated IVDs. In addition, the uncertainty maps generated by our model provide valuable insights for clinical review, enhancing the robustness and reliability of the segmentation results. The potential of diffusion models to enhance the diagnosis and management of LBP through more precise analysis of pathological spine MRI is underscored by our findings.

</details>


### [92] [An active-learning framework for real-time depth perception from monocular vision streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04917)
*Xiaorong Zeng,Weiqiang Chen,Peng Shi,Liang Su,Zirui Wang,Xuewu Ji,Shuiwen Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线主动学习（OAL）机制，结合弹性权重巩固（EWC）实现选择性可塑性，使轻量级神经网络（基于MobileNetV3-Small）在部署后能够自适应流式单目视觉输入进行深度估计，在降低约75%计算成本的同时保持有竞争力的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级网络在静态离线训练后部署即冻结，面对域偏移时性能严重退化；而大规模模型虽然参数冗余但计算负担大，紧凑模型强行学习通用几何表示会导致计算效率低下和性能饱和。作者受生物视觉系统在动态环境中平衡稳定性与可塑性的启发，试图解决这一静态优化困境。

Method: 引入在线主动学习（OAL）机制，建立闭环的"预测-评估-纠正"（Predict-Evaluate-Correct）学习范式，从流式视觉输入中主动选择高置信度、信息丰富的信号。利用弹性权重巩固（EWC）实现选择性可塑性：保留编码全局相关结构知识的参数，同时允许对新型环境进行局部对齐。系统基于MobileNetV3-Small骨干网络构建。

Result: 实验结果表明该系统在保持有竞争力的深度估计精度的同时，计算成本降低了约75%。关键发现是：适应性并非仅由模型大小决定，而是依赖于如何在动态环境中有效调控参数可塑性。

Conclusion: 通过合理的可塑性调控机制，即便是轻量级网络也能在资源受限的边缘设备上实现持续在线适应，为边缘部署的深度感知模型提供了新的解决方案思路。

Abstract: Biological visual systems can perceive depth from monocular vision flow, continuously integrating temporal visual cues while maintaining a balance between stability and plasticity in dynamic environments. In contrast, artificial perception models deployed on resource-constrained edge devices are typically trained in a static offline manner and remain frozen after deployment, often suffering severe performance degradation under domain shifts. While large-scale models may encode broad knowledge through massive parameter redundancy, lightweight networks face a static optimization dilemma: forcing compact models to learn universal geometric representations is computationally inefficient and often leads to performance saturation. To resolve this issue, an Online Active Learning (OAL) mechanism is introduced to endow compact neural networks with the capability to adapt continuously during operation. A closed-loop Predict-Evaluate-Correct learning paradigm is established to actively select high-confidence, information-rich signals from streaming visual input. Crucially, Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) is employed not merely to prevent catastrophic forgetting, but to enforce Selective Plasticity, preserving parameters that encode globally relevant structural knowledge while allowing local alignment to newly observed environments. Built upon a MobileNetV3-Small backbone, the proposed system achieves approximately a 75% reduction in computational cost while maintaining competitive depth estimation accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that adaptability is not solely determined by model size, but rather by how effectively parameter plasticity is regulated in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [93] [Unleashing the Potential of Vision-Language Models for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04935)
*Weihan Cai,Hao Tan,Zichang Tan,Jun Wan,Xinping Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义原型校准（SPC）方法，利用视觉-语言模型感知编码器（PE）的语言对齐表征进行AIGI检测。PE在冻结特征空间中比DINOv3展现出更强的局部溯源语义组织，但线性探测无法利用该结构，因此SPC通过构建类别原型并结合监督数据进行校准，最终PE-SPC在跨生成器、后处理及野外场景基准上超越DINOv3基线，取得新的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明线性探测在DINOv3等视觉基础模型上可达到SOTA的AIGI检测效果，但作者发现DINOv3并非最优选择——感知编码器（PE）因具备语言对齐表征而保留了更丰富的高层溯源语义，且其冻结特征空间中具有更强的局部溯源组织性。然而语义无关的线性探测无法充分利用这种结构化信息，导致PE-Linear仍落后于DINOv3-Linear 4.1%，因此需要设计能够挖掘PE中溯源语义结构的检测方法。

Method: 提出语义原型校准（SPC）方法：从取证语义信息中构建类别原型，并通过监督数据对原型进行校准。将SPC应用于感知编码器PE，得到检测器PE-SPC。该方法利用PE语言对齐表征中的溯源语义结构，而非依赖语义无关的线性探测。

Result: PE-SPC在跨生成器、后处理和野外场景（In-the-Wild）等基准测试中均超越了此前的DINOv3基线，取得了新的最先进（SOTA）结果，展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 感知编码器（PE）作为视觉-语言模型，在AIGI检测中比DINOv3具有更大的潜力，其关键优势在于语言对齐表征保留的高层溯源语义。通过SPC方法利用这一结构特性，简单设计即可实现更强的检测泛化性能，为AIGI检测提供了新的基础模型选择和研究方向。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that a simple linear probe on frozen representations from modern vision foundation models (VFMs) can achieve state-of-the-art AIGI detection performance, substantially outperforming specialized detectors in challenging in-the-wild scenarios. This finding has established DINOv3 as the dominant foundation-model baseline for subsequent improvements. However, we find that the vision-language model Perception Encoder (PE) holds greater potential for AIGI detection, because its language-aligned representation preserves high-level provenance semantics. Specifically, PE exhibits stronger local provenance organization than DINOv3 in its frozen feature space. However, semantic-agnostic linear probing fails to exploit this structure, as PE-Linear still underperforms DINOv3-Linear by 4.1% on In-the-Wild. Based on this observation, we propose Semantic Prototype Calibration (SPC), which constructs category prototypes from forensic semantic information and calibrates them with supervised data. We apply SPC to PE and refer to the resulting detector as PE-SPC. Our analysis shows that this simple design achieves stronger generalization. Across cross-generator, post-processing, and in-the-wild benchmarks, PE-SPC surpasses the previous DINOv3 baseline and achieves new state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [94] [Towards Valid B-Rep Generation: Training-Free Wireframe Anomaly Detection and Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04955)
*Jingyu Wu,Youcheng Cai,Tengyu Luo,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WDR（Wireframe Detection and Repair）框架，这是一个无需训练的中间产物级干预方案，用于检测和修复多阶段B-Rep生成过程中线框阶段的几何与拓扑异常，从而提升最终CAD模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段B-Rep生成依赖中间线框合成CAD模型，但线框中存在的几何和拓扑风险（如自交、边塌缩、顶点断开）会传播导致最终B-Rep无效。通过重新训练大规模生成模型来缓解这些失败在计算上代价高昂，因此需要一种无需训练的干预方案。

Method: WDR包含两个核心模块：(1) 几何-拓扑异常检测器（GTAD）结合并行VLM粗筛和几何/拓扑检测器来预测下游无效风险，并路由生成到专用分支；(2) 能量引导的几何-拓扑修复模块（EGGTR）通过几何和拓扑分支执行由检测器触发的引导重生成。通过能量引导重采样和无需训练的自由引导扩展到扩散模型，WDR可无缝集成到自回归和扩散流水线中。

Result: 大量实验表明，WDR在保持合成CAD模型多样性和分布质量的同时，一致性地提升了内核校验的有效性。

Conclusion: WDR通过在线框阶段进行检测与修复，以低成本有效改善了B-Rep生成的有效性，且无需重新训练即可适配不同生成流水线。

Abstract: Multi-stage boundary representation (B-Rep) generation leverages intermediate wireframes to synthesize CAD models. However, geometric and topological risks in these wireframes -- such as self-intersections, edge collapses, and disconnected vertices -- can propagate to invalid final B-Reps. Mitigating such failures by retraining large generative models is computationally prohibitive. We propose Wireframe Detection and Repair (WDR), a training-free framework that intervenes at the intermediate wireframe stage to improve downstream B-Rep validity. WDR features a Geometric-Topology Anomaly Detector (GTAD) that combines parallel VLM-based coarse screening with geometric and topological detectors to predict downstream invalidity risk and route generation to dedicated branches. An Energy-Guided Geometric-Topology Repair (EGGTR) module then performs detector-triggered guided regeneration through geometry and topology branches. By scaling test-time computation via Energy-Guided Resampling and training-free guidance for diffusion models, WDR can be integrated into autoregressive and diffusion pipelines without retraining. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in kernel-checked validity while largely retaining the measured diversity and distributional quality of synthesized CAD models. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [95] [ContextMaster: Interactive Multi-Shot Video Creation via Fixed-Budget Sparse Context Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04956)
*Xu Guo,Zhengxuan Wei,Xinghui Li,Hanzhuo Huang,Xinyu Liu,Xiangyang Luo,Min Wei,Yiran Zhu,Qiulin Wang,Yulong Xu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Qi Fan,Xiangwang Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextMaster，一个统一的多镜头视频生成模型，支持文本生成、参考条件生成和视频编辑三种操作，通过角色感知的上下文表示、固定预算的稀疏上下文路由和特权上下文蒸馏框架，实现高效且一致的多镜头视频创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型虽然能同时支持生成、参考条件和编辑，但通常将它们视为对固定输入的独立操作。实际创作需要跨多个镜头（multi-shot）进行，要求一个模型在不同操作间保持共享历史信息，这是一个尚未被充分研究的设定。

Method: 1）提出IMVC（交互式多镜头视频创作）形式化设定；2）ContextMaster模型包含角色感知上下文表示、可复用干净上下文状态与固定预算稀疏上下文路由、ConstraintSink保持任务约束可见；3）提出两阶段特权上下文蒸馏框架：从密集教师模型通过一致性蒸馏迁移完整上下文行为，再通过分布匹配优化部署rollout。

Result: 在三种基础任务（生成、参考条件、编辑）上优于专用基线，用户研究验证了灵活组合工作流的有效性，模型在单GPU上达到16FPS的推理速度。

Conclusion: ContextMaster证明了统一模型在稀疏上下文访问和少步采样条件下仍能保持跨镜头的任务完成度和一致性，为交互式多镜头视频创作提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent video models increasingly support generation, reference conditioning, and editing within a single model, yet typically expose them as separate operations over fixed inputs. Practical creation unfolds across multiple shots, requiring one model to generate from text, follow a reference, or edit source footage while maintaining shared history. We formalize this setting as interactive multi-shot video creation (IMVC) and introduce ContextMaster, a unified model with a role-aware context representation for these operations. An interactive model must retain access to an expanding history without allowing the context read cost at each denoising step to grow. ContextMaster combines reusable clean context states with fixed budget sparse context routing and uses ConstraintSink to keep task constraints visible. To address the dual challenges of sparse context access and inference with few denoising steps, we propose a two-stage privileged context distillation framework, which transfers full context behavior from a dense teacher through consistency distillation and then refines deployment rollouts with distribution matching. Experiments on the three primitive tasks demonstrate improved task fulfillment and consistency across shots over specialized baselines. User studies further validate flexibly composed workflows, while the model reaches 16 FPS on a single GPU.

</details>


### [96] [Promptable Animal Pose Tracking Across Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04995)
*Le Li,Daniela Ivanova,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于视觉基础模型的动物姿态跟踪方法（无监督和有监督），用于在标注数据有限的情况下跟踪用户选定的关键点，在APTv2和TigDog基准上取得了准确性和泛化性之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物姿态估计与追踪对野生动物监测和保护研究至关重要，但专家标注时间有限。现有方法要么依赖标注数据集（如APTv2）但泛化能力差，要么用视觉追踪追踪自定义关键点但性能受损。需要一种在有限标注下既准确又具有跨物种泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出两个模型：（1）监督方法：通过关键点提示编码器从参考帧显式注入结构先验到特征匹配中，实现更优的跟踪精度；（2）无监督方法：利用多样化基础模型特征进行免训练对应匹配，提供强大的跨物种鲁棒性。

Result: 在APTv2和TigDog两个具有挑战性的动物视频基准上进行广泛评估，该框架取得了强性能，同时保持了准确性与泛化性之间的有效平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为真实世界的动物行为分析和保护应用提供了实用解决方案，在标注数据有限的情况下实现了有效的动物姿态跟踪。

Abstract: Animal pose estimation and tracking is important for wildlife monitoring and conservation research, and with limited expert time for labelling automated approaches are imperative. While human pose estimation and tracking has seen rapid progress thanks to large annotated datasets, animal pose remain challenging, due to large morphological and behavioural differences between species and limited annotated data. Existing approaches either optimise generic keypoint localisation from annotated datasets (such as APTv2) with poor generalisation, or track custom keypoints using visual tracking, at the cost of performance. In this paper, we demonstrate that vision foundation models trained on large datasets can be used effectively to track animal pose with limited labelled data. We propose two models, one unsupervised and the other supervised, to track user-selected keypoints in videos. The supervised approach delivers superior tracking accuracy by employing a keypoint prompt encoder to explicitly inject structural priors from a reference frame into feature matching. In parallel, the unsupervised route provides strong cross-species robustness by leveraging diverse foundation-model features for training-free correspondence matching. Extensive evaluation on challenging animal video benchmarks APTv2 and TigDog demonstrates that our framework achieves strong performance while maintaining an effective balance between accuracy and generalisation, offering a practical solution for real-world animal behaviour analysis and conservation applications.

</details>


### [97] [Towards Physics of Multimodal Pretraining: Knowledge Flow, Modality Synergy, Early Unification, and Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05000)
*Junlin Han,Shengbang Tong,David Fan,Minghao Chen,Philip Torr,Filippos Kokkinos,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性对照实验探究多模态预训练的内在机制，基于合成数据和大规模真实数据得出四大关键发现：知识流动模式、数据复杂度决定模态协同与竞争、早期统一训练优于晚期对齐（揭示"视觉懒惰"现象）、以及仅用5%计算预算的高效预训练配方，并在13.5B MoE模型和2T token规模上验证了这些结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态统一预训练已成为推动基础模型发展的重要方向，但模态间在统一训练中如何交互的设计空间和基本机制仍未被充分探索，缺乏系统性的实证理解。

Method: 通过在合成数据和大型真实世界数据集上开展受控实验，系统性地探究多模态预训练；具体包括解耦语言、视觉理解和视觉生成之间的知识迁移，分析数据复杂度对模态协同/竞争的影响，对比早期统一与晚期对齐/顺序训练策略，并评估不同视觉tokenizer设计及架构选择（如共享注意力、归一化与模态特定前馈层）的作用。

Result: 四大发现：(i)语言、视觉理解和视觉生成之间存在不同的知识迁移模式和非对称影响；(ii)数据复杂度决定模态间是协同还是竞争，共享注意力和归一化配合模态特定前馈层能促进协同，且在不同视觉tokenizer上表现一致；(iii)早期统一联合训练优于晚期对齐或顺序训练，并发现"视觉懒惰"现象（延迟集成导致模型依赖语言先验）；(iv)推导出仅需5%计算预算即可获得强生成性能的高效预训练配方。这些发现在多13.5B MoE模型和2T token规模上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和扩展多模态预训练提供了原则性基础，明确了知识流动、模态协同条件、统一训练时机和高效配方等关键机制，为未来多模态基础模型的设计和规模扩展提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Vision offers a critical axis for advancing foundation models, driving a shift towards natively unified multimodal pretraining. Despite this momentum, the design space and the fundamental mechanisms of how modalities interact during unified training remain underexplored. We provide empirical clarity through a systematic exploration of multimodal pretraining. Our controlled experiments on both synthetic and large-scale real-world datasets yield four key insights into the physics of multimodal pretraining: (i) Knowledge Flow: We disentangle how language, visual understanding, and visual generation transfer knowledge across modalities, revealing distinct patterns of influence and asymmetry; (ii) Synergy vs. Competition: We show that data "complexity" largely determines whether modalities are synergistic, identify architectural choices that promote synergy: such as shared attention and normalization with modality-specific feed-forward layers, and find that these behaviors generalize across different visual tokenizer designs; (iii) Early Unification: Unifying modalities from the very early stages and training them jointly is shown to be more effective than late alignment or sequential training. This process uncovers a vision laziness phenomenon, where delayed integration leads models to rely on language priors; (iv) Recipes: We derive efficient pretraining recipes that achieve strong generative performance using only 5% of the compute budget. These core findings are subsequently validated at scale by training multiple 13.5B MoE models on 2T tokens. We hope this study provides a principled foundation for understanding and scaling multimodal pretraining.

</details>


### [98] [OmniEdit-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Instruction-based Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05049)
*Chenxuan Miao,Yutong Feng,Yi Lu,Yunfeng Yan,Donglian Qi,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Xi Chen,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniEdit-Bench，一个针对指令式视频编辑（IVE）的全面结构化评测基准。该基准将编辑任务分解为空间、时间、音频和参考编辑等多个视频特有维度，区分显式和隐式指令并纳入推理场景；同时提出包含准确性、保持性、真实性和一致性四个维度的评估框架，并引入准确性惩罚机制来强调指令遵从度，防止视觉上合理但实际错误的编辑获得虚高评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑评测基准存在两大局限：一是任务覆盖有限，主要继承自图像编辑，忽略了视频特有的编辑维度（如时间、音频等）；二是评估指标不充分，无法衡量指令遵从度，导致错误编辑因原始视频的强视觉先验而获得高分。

Method: 1) 构建结构化评测基准，将编辑任务分解为空间、时间、音频和基于参考的编辑等视频特有维度；2) 区分显式和隐式指令，并纳入基于推理的编辑场景；3) 提出四维度评估框架（准确性、保持性、真实性、一致性），结合人工判断和先进视觉语言模型；4) 引入准确性惩罚机制，以准确性为条件约束其他评分。

Result: 在代表性开源和商业模型上的大量实验表明，当前IVE模型的表现远未达到令人满意的水平，揭示了现有视频编辑模型在指令遵循方面的显著不足。

Conclusion: OmniEdit-Bench为指令式视频编辑评估提供了全面可靠的测试平台，并为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Instruction-based video editing (IVE) is an emerging field with broad applications, yet evaluating editing models remains challenging. Existing benchmarks suffer from two major limitations: limited task coverage inherited from image editing, which overlooks video-specific dimensions, and inadequate metrics that fail to measure instruction fidelity, allowing incorrect edits to receive high scores due to strong visual priors from the original video. To address these issues, we introduce a comprehensive and structured benchmark for IVE. Our benchmark decomposes editing tasks into multiple video-specific dimensions, including spatial, temporal, audio, and reference-based editing, extending beyond conventional frame-level evaluation. It also distinguishes explicit and implicit instructions and incorporates reasoning-based scenarios to better reflect real-world requirements. Furthermore, we propose an evaluation framework that assesses editing quality from four complementary dimensions: accuracy, preservation, realism, and consistency, using both human judgments and state-of-the-art vision-language models. To emphasize instruction fidelity, we introduce an accuracy-aware penalty mechanism that conditions other scores on accuracy, preventing visually plausible but incorrect edits from receiving inflated evaluations. Extensive experiments on representative open-source and commercial models show that current IVE models remain far from satisfactory. OmniEdit-Bench provides a comprehensive and reliable testbed for evaluating instruction-based video editing and offers insights into future research directions.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Reprojection Error: Camera Calibration with 3D Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05066)
*Dennis Ruppel,Hasan Kutlu,Kai A. Neumann,Martin Knuth,Pedro Santos,Andreas Weinmann,Arjan Kuijper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向3D重建的相机标定框架，基于场景光线预测而非传统2D平面标定，并引入了重建误差和相交误差两种新指标，配合自助采样法评估不同标定对象和流程。同时设计了二十面体标定靶及环特征检测器，实验表明该方法在合成数据上可将平均相交误差降低约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于2D平面标定的方法在3D重建场景中存在局限性，重投影误差无法全面反映3D空间中的标定精度，且缺乏对标定对象和流程的系统性评估手段，因此需要更灵活、更贴合3D重建需求的标定框架。

Method: 提出基于场景光线预测的标定框架，利用广义畸变模型拟合物理相机效应；定义重建误差和相交误差两种新指标，结合自助采样法统计评估不同标定对象与流程的内外参标定效果；设计二十面体标定靶并配套环特征检测器以丰富3D重建的标定信息。

Result: 广义畸变模型能更忠实捕捉物理相机效应并提升标定精度；重投影误差被证明在评估3D精度时可能产生误导，而基于光线的指标更为全面；二十面体标定靶在合成数据上平均相交误差降低约40%，标定稳定性更高，但真实数据需要极高的制造公差。

Conclusion: 基于场景光线的标定框架为3D重建提供了更灵活、更准确的标定方案，所提指标和二十面体标定目标具有明显优势，但其实际应用对硬件制造精度有较高要求。

Abstract: In 3D reconstruction, camera calibration is an essential element for achieving high fidelity and accuracy of the reconstructed geometry. While existing approaches rely upon 2D planar calibration, this work proposes a framework tailored for 3D reconstruction that is based on predicting scene rays, which adds flexibility to the reconstruction pipeline and enables the use of recent advances in camera models. Novel metrics, reconstruction and intersection error, derived from predicted scene rays are employed in combination with a bootstrapping procedure that statistically evaluates different calibration objects and calibration pipelines for both intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. The results show that the generalized distortion model more faithfully captures physical camera effects and yields an improvement in calibration accuracy. Reprojection error is shown to be a potentially misleading indicator of 3D accuracy, and the proposed ray-based metrics provide a more holistic assessment. An icosahedron calibration target is designed to enrich calibration information for 3D reconstruction together with a ring-feature-based detector. The icosahedral target yields approximately 40% lower mean intersection and more stable calibration across bootstrap trials on synthetic data, while real-data performance demands very tight fabrication tolerances.

</details>


### [100] [VQ-VAD: Vector-quantized Motion Representation Learning for Human-centric Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05069)
*Narges Rashvand,Ghazal Alinezhad Noghre,Shanle Yao,Gabriel Maldonado,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VQ-VAD，一种基于离散运动表示的人体中心视频异常检测框架，通过将VQ-GAN从图像生成任务适配到关键点序列，构建正常行为的运动码本，并通过重建误差检测异常，在四个基准上验证了领域内、跨领域和跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于姿态的视频异常检测（pose-based VAD）在连续潜空间中建模人类行为，难以学习紧凑的运动模式，从而限制了行为分析的鲁棒性。现有方法未能充分利用离散表示来捕获正常行为的典型运动模式。

Method: VQ-VAD将向量量化生成对抗网络（VQ-GAN）从图像生成领域适配到关键点序列处理，构建正常行为的运动码本（motion codebook）。仅使用正常运动序列进行训练，通过计算观测运动序列与学得码本之间的重建误差来判断异常。

Result: 在四个异常检测基准上进行了领域内、跨领域和跨数据集三种互补的评估设置。领域内准确率达到81.83%（HR-SHT），跨领域从CMU Panoptic迁移到HR-SHT无需重训练即达76.69%，跨数据集鲁棒性也表现良好。

Conclusion: VQ-VAD验证了离散运动表示在视频异常检测中的有效性，相比连续潜空间方法能更好地学习紧凑运动模式，同时具备良好的跨领域和跨数据集泛化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) is inherently challenging due to the scarcity of anomalies and the large visual variability in surveillance footage, including changes in lighting, viewpoint, and human appearance. To mitigate visual noise and address privacy concerns, recent work has shifted to pose-based VAD, which focuses on motion dynamics rather than raw video data. However, existing pose-based approaches model human behavior in continuous latent spaces, limiting their ability to learn compact motion patterns necessary for robust behavior analysis. We address this by proposing Vector-Quantized Video Anomaly Detection (VQ-VAD), a novel human-centric anomaly detection framework that learns discrete motion representations. VQ-VAD adapts Vector-Quantized GAN (VQ-GAN), originally developed for image generation, to operate on keypoint sequences and construct a motion codebook of normal behavior. Trained exclusively on normal motion sequences, VQ-VAD detects anomalies by identifying high reconstruction errors when an observed motion sequence cannot be mapped to the learned codebook. We conduct extensive experiments across three complementary evaluation settings, including in-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset generalization, on four anomaly detection benchmarks. VQ-VAD achieves strong in-domain accuracy (81.83% on HR-SHT [15]), effective cross-domain transfer from CMU Panoptic [14] (76.69% on HR-SHT [15] without retraining), and competitive cross-dataset robustness. The code base for this work is available at https://github.com/TeCSAR-UNCC/VQ-VAD.

</details>


### [101] [HelloWorld: Enabling Socially Interactive Characters in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05070)
*Liangyang Ouyang,Ruicong Liu,Xuangeng Chu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelloWorld是一个支持用户与视频世界内角色进行社交互动的视频世界模型。用户按下按钮即可提示屏幕角色向镜头做出回应（如转身、挥手、点头或问候）。通过自蒸馏pipeline微调视频生成模型，并引入训练-free模块在推理时定位交互发生的时间窗口。基于400样本的HelloWorldBench基准评估，该模型在交互质量上超越多种基线，同时保持最先进的画面美学和摄像机姿态跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频世界模型近期取得了显著进展，但用户与视频世界中角色之间的社交互动仍未被支持。现有模型只能生成视频内容，无法实现用户与角色的实时交互，这限制了视频世界模型的沉浸感和实用价值。

Method: 1) 提出自蒸馏pipeline：视频生成模型在自身合成的数据上进行微调，每个合成片段同时包含社交互动和摄像机运动，使模型在不降低交互质量的前提下学习摄像机姿态条件化。2) 提出训练-free的交互时间定位模块：在推理时按下按钮后，调制DiT的交叉注意力掩码，使交互相关文本提示仅关注按钮按下窗口内的帧，从而在时间上定位角色的回应。

Result: 在交互质量上超越了多种基线方法，同时在画面美学和摄像机姿态跟随方面保持了最先进的性能。构建了HelloWorldBench基准（400个样本），包含三个社交交互指标和三个传统指标用于评估。

Conclusion: HelloWorld首次实现了视频世界模型中的用户与角色社交交互功能，通过自蒸馏和训练-free注意力调制两大核心创新，在保持生成质量的同时大幅提升交互自然度，为视频世界模型的人机交互提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable recent progress of video world models, social interaction between users and the characters within these worlds remains unsupported. To fill this gap, we present HelloWorld, a video world model that enables social interaction with in-world characters. With a single button press, users can prompt the on-screen character to respond toward the camera, e.g., turning to the viewer, waving, nodding, or speaking a short greeting. To make these interactions natural, we propose a self-distillation pipeline that finetunes the video generation model on data synthesized by itself. Each synthesized clip contains both social interactions and camera motion, allowing the model to learn camera-pose conditioning without degrading interaction quality. At inference, we further introduce a training-free module that determines when the interaction occurs. Upon a button press, it modulates the cross-attention masks of the DiT so that the interaction-related text prompt attends only to the frames within the press window, temporally localizing the character's response. We further build HelloWorldBench, a 400-sample benchmark with three social interaction metrics alongside three conventional metrics, for evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that HelloWorld surpasses a variety of baselines in interaction quality, while maintaining state-of-the-art picture aesthetics and camera-pose following. Project page: https://github.com/AlayaLab/HelloWorld

</details>


### [102] [Bag-of-Visual-Words for Spatial Mapping of Lung Adenocarcinoma Growth Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05074)
*Darya Ardan,Valentin Oreiller,Henning Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于词袋模型（BoVW）的弱监督管道，利用冻结的基石模型嵌入和少量标注ROI构建视觉词汇表，通过最近原型分类生成肺腺癌（LUAD）全切片图像（WSI）的空间生长模式图谱。在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上评估，肿瘤/健康分类最佳平衡准确率达0.974（接近监督基线0.987），组织学分级分类在所有编码器上均优于监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LUAD空间生长模式映射方法在单个tile层面操作，产生的是通用的形态学聚类而非临床定义的图谱，缺乏区域级别的架构上下文解析能力，无法生成可解释的临床模式图。

Method: 提出弱监督Bag-of-Visual-Words（BoVW）管道：从少量标注ROI提取冻结的基石模型嵌入构建视觉词汇表；将同标签ROI的BoVW直方图均值作为模式原型；使用Jensen-Shannon散度进行滑动窗口区域的最近原型分类；将预测投影到WSI tile网格生成空间模式图谱。

Result: 在87例CPTAC-LUAD患者上使用三种基石模型编码器和多种词汇表大小进行评估。肿瘤/健康分类最佳配置平衡准确率0.974（H-Optimus-1），接近监督SVM基线0.987；二元组织学分级分类中，BoVW管道在所有编码器上均优于基于全局均值池化的监督基线。

Conclusion: RoI级别的模式分解能够保留全局均值池化所衰减的、与分级相关的异质性信息，BoVW管道为LUAD空间图谱提供了一种可解释且有效的弱监督方案。

Abstract: Spatial mapping of lung adenocarcinoma (LUAD) growth patterns across whole slide images (WSIs) requires resolving architectural context at the region level, yet existing methods operate at the individual tile level and produce generic morphological clusters rather than clinically defined pattern maps. We propose a weakly supervised Bag-of-Visual-Words (BoVW) pipeline that learns a visual vocabulary from frozen foundation model embeddings extracted from a small set of annotated regions of interest (ROIs). Pattern prototypes are constructed as mean BoVW histograms of same-label ROIs and used for nearest-prototype classification of sliding-window regions under Jensen--Shannon divergence. The resulting predictions are projected onto the WSI tile grid to produce interpretable spatial pattern maps. We evaluate the method on 87 CPTAC-LUAD patients using three foundation model encoders and multiple vocabulary sizes on two clinically motivated tasks. For tumour/healthy classification, the best configuration achieves a balanced accuracy of $0.974$ with H-Optimus-1, approaching the $0.987$ obtained by a supervised SVM trained on mean-pooled WSI embeddings. For binary histologic grade classification, the BoVW pipeline achieves higher balanced accuracy than the supervised baseline for all encoders, suggesting that ROI-level pattern decomposition preserves grade-relevant heterogeneity that is attenuated by global mean pooling.

</details>


### [103] [Lesion Detection in CT with Frozen Self-Distilled Features: SALT, a Spatially Adaptive Label-Guided Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05100)
*Mahmut S. Gokmen,Evan W. Damron,Mitchell A. Klusty,Caroline N. Leach,Emily B. Collier,V. K. Cody Bumgardner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT（Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature）方法，通过空间自适应地调节自蒸馏目标（教师温度锐化+掩码补丁损失加权），使自监督预训练在病灶区域产生更强的训练信号，而不改变模型架构或推理时的标签条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督预训练目标在空间上是均匀的，病灶区域（仅几个patch大小）对训练信号的贡献与周围正常实质相当，导致模型对病灶区域不够敏感。已有工作仅对视图进行标签引导裁剪，改变了模型看到的内容但未对目标函数施加额外压力。

Method: SALT利用预训练阶段可获得的弱标签（如bounding box），在编码器的patch网格上定义一个紧凑区域。在该区域内：（1）教师softmax温度被锐化；（2）掩码patch损失被加权上调。目标函数、掩码策略和中心化统计保持不变。下游使用时编码器是普通的特征提取器，无需标签或条件化。

Result: 在四个CT队列的3D病灶检测中，冻结编码器仅训练轻量级多深度CenterNet风格头部。报告了patch级可分性、按队列和病灶大小分层的3D检测结果、框质量和无检测器探针（单一冻结patch嵌入在无配准/掩码/微调情况下于随访扫描中重新识别病灶）。与架构、预训练数据、调度和标签引导裁剪相同但无目标条件化的基线相比，验证了机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 由于条件化通过空间指示器而非标签语义表达，该方法可适配任意弱空间标注。本文在病灶检测场景中实例化并验证了该框架，证明了空间自适应目标条件化能有效提升预训练对局部病理区域的表征能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining objectives are spatially uniform: the teacher temperature and the per-patch loss weight are identical everywhere in the image, so a lesion a few patches wide contributes no more to the training signal than the surrounding parenchyma. Prior work biases the views toward annotated regions, which changes what the model sees but adds no pressure on the objective. We instead condition the targets of self-distillation, a method we call SALT (Spatially Adaptive Label-guided Temperature). Weak, box-derived labels, available only during pretraining, define a compact region on the encoder's patch grid, inside which the teacher's softmax temperature is sharpened and the masked-patch loss is up-weighted. The objectives, the masking policy and the centering statistics are otherwise unchanged, and at every downstream use the encoder is a plain feature extractor with no labels and no conditioning. We evaluate by freezing the encoder and training only a lightweight multi-depth CenterNet-style head, detecting lesions in 3D on four CT cohorts, and we isolate the mechanism against a backbone identical in architecture, pretraining data, schedule and label-guided cropping but with no target conditioning. We report patch-level separability, 3D detection stratified by cohort and by lesion size, box quality, and a detector-free probe in which a single frozen patch embedding re-identifies a lesion in a follow-up scan without registration, masks or fine-tuning. Because the conditioning is expressed through a spatial indicator rather than through label semantics, the formulation admits any weak spatial annotation; we instantiate and validate it for lesions.

</details>


### [104] [HexMIL: Hierarchical Attention MIL for Ante-Hoc Explainable Detection of AI-Manipulated CT Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05101)
*Orazio Pontorno,Luca Guarnera,Zahid Akhtar,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HexMIL，一种基于层级可解释多实例学习（MIL）的无掩膜医学深伪检测器，仅使用二元体积极标签即可同时实现跨生成架构的泛化检测和像素级定位。通过两层级（patch和slice）的Gated Attention聚合，将注意力权重直接组合为全分辨率3D注意力体，实现前验（ante-hoc）且结构忠实的结构化空间归因。在M3DSynth和CT-GAN数据集上的跨生成器测试中，HexMIL在分类和定位任务上均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学深伪图像（由深度生成模型操纵的医学图像）严重威胁临床工作流，但现有检测器存在两大关键缺陷：（1）对未见过的生成架构泛化能力差；（2）缺乏可解释性。作者旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: HexMIL将每个CT体积分解为patch和slice两个层级的层级结构，通过独立的Gated Attention模块进行聚合，并将注意力权重直接组合成全分辨率3D注意力体，用于定位被操纵的子区域。与Grad-CAM等事后（post-hoc）方法不同，HexMIL的注意力权重正是驱动分类决策的精确前向计算结果，提供了前验（ante-hoc）且结构忠实的空间归因，且无需任何像素级标注。

Result: 在M3DSynth和CT-GAN数据集上，采用严格的跨生成器泛化协议（在单一生成架构上训练、在未见架构上测试），HexMIL在域外分类中比所有基线高出+9.1 AUC和+9.4 F1，并在定位任务上取得最佳平均IoU和Pointing Game分数。

Conclusion: HexMIL通过层级多实例学习框架，仅利用二元体积极标签就同时实现了对未见生成架构的良好泛化和无需像素标注的精确定位，为医学深伪检测提供了一种兼具鲁棒性和可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: The emergence of medical deepfakes, i.e., medical images manipulated by deep generative models, poses a significant threat to clinical workflows. However, existing detectors suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to unseen generative architectures for manipulation detection and lack of interpretability. In this context, we present HexMIL (Hierarchical EXplainable Multiple Instance Learning), a mask-free medical deepfake detector that simultaneously addresses both limitations using only binary volume-level supervision. HexMIL decomposes each CT volume into a two-level hierarchy of patches and slices, aggregated via independent Gated Attention modules whose weights are directly combined into a full-resolution 3D attention volume that localizes the manipulated sub-region without any pixel-level annotation. Unlike post-hoc methods such as Grad-CAM, HexMIL's attention weights constitute the exact forward computation driving the classification decision, providing ante-hoc and structurally faithful spatial attribution. We evaluate HexMIL on M3DSynth and CT-GAN datasets under a rigorous cross-generator generalization protocol, training on a single generative architecture and testing on unseen ones. HexMIL outperforms all baselines by $+9.1$ AUC and $+9.4$ F1 in out-of-domain classification, and achieves the best average IoU and Pointing Game score in localization. Project page: opontorno.github.io/hexmil.

</details>


### [105] [Robust and Efficient Motion Reasoning for Privacy-Aware Classroom Incident Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05115)
*Paritosh Parmar,Landy Lan,Hong Yang,Chen Yi,Chiat Pin Tay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合生成式CCTV风格视频与真实课堂姿态数据的混合基准，并设计了一种轻量级运动推理框架，通过层次化运动学表征和知识蒸馏，实现隐私保护且高效的课堂事故识别，在不到基线十分之一计算成本下取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂事故识别这一场景未被充分探索，缺乏针对隐私保护、计算效率和泛化能力要求的基准与方法。现有方法忽略了事故在运动方向、速度、加速度和强度上的差异，而非仅姿态差异。

Method: 1) 构建混合基准：结合生成式CCTV风格视频与真实课堂姿态数据；2) 构建层次化运动学表征（hierarchical kinematic representations）描述人类动作；3) 通过知识蒸馏，将大教师模型的多阶运动推理能力压缩为小且高效的单阶学生模型，实现逐人高效推理。

Result: 模型在不到基线十分之一计算成本下超越更大规模的基线模型，展现出更强的跨域运动推理能力和零样本合成到真实的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为隐私感知的课堂安全提供了可行的轻量级解决方案，并将公开发布基准、代码和相关工具以推动该领域进一步研究。

Abstract: Can computer vision help make classrooms safer? In this pilot study, we investigate privacy-aware and computationally efficient classroom incident recognition from CCTV-style observations. This setting remains underexplored, with limited benchmarks and few methods designed for the privacy, efficiency, and generalization demands of real-world deployment. We introduce a novel hybrid benchmark combining generative CCTV-style videos with real-world classroom pose data, and propose a lightweight, but robust motion-reasoning framework motivated by the observation that many incidents differ more in motion direction, speed, acceleration, and intensity than in pose alone. To that end, our method first constructs hierarchical kinematic representations of human actions. Our method then distills hierarchical, multi-order kinematic reasoning from a large teacher into a much smaller single-order student, enabling efficient per-person inference while preserving expressive motion understanding. Experiments show that our model outperforms substantially larger baselines at less than one-tenth of their computational cost, while also demonstrating stronger out-of-domain motion reasoning and zero-shot synthetic-to-real generalization. We will publicly release the benchmark, codebase, and supporting tools to facilitate further research in privacy-aware classroom safety.

</details>


### [106] [IRIS: A Visual Cortex-Inspired Framework for Analyzing Orientation Selectivity in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05122)
*Vaishnavi B Mohan,Vijayakrishna Naganoor,Yashas Annadani,Shashank Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入神经科学启发的度量指标（RSS、ORS、方向调谐带宽），系统研究了视觉Transformer（ViT）中方向选择性的涌现机制，发现训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，并揭示了不同深度层在训练过程中方向选择性的动态变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer已成为感知任务的标准图像编码器，但其缺乏归纳偏置，机制上尚不清楚它们如何编码低级特征（如方向选择性）。生物视觉系统通过局部感受野构建低级特征，这些特征是跨通路共享的通用表示。因此作者想探究这种生物学基础的特征是否在ViT中涌现，以及如何涌现。

Method: 提出了三种神经科学启发的度量指标：表示相似性评分（RSS）、方向招募评分（ORS）和方向调谐带宽，用于量化方向选择性在表示几何中及其随模型深度的编码方式。通过对ViT进行系统分析，考察训练范式、模型规模和深度等因素对方向选择性涌现的影响。

Result: 主要发现：（1）训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，共享目标的模型在相对深度上达到峰值，与规模无关；（2）许多单元在训练早期就具有方向选择性，早期到中层随时间招募更多此类单元，而深层则丧失选择性并向语义编码拓宽；（3）度量指标可作为解冻层数的最佳泛化启发式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在ViT训练过程中追踪生物学基础特征的方法，能够探测所需属性如何在Transformer表示中被编码，并系统性地理解ViT跨任务的泛化机制。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have become the de facto standard for image encoding across many perception tasks. Despite their empirical success, it remains mechanistically unclear how they encode low-level features, given their lack of inductive biases: ViTs process information globally rather than relying on local structure. Biological visual systems, in contrast, build low-level features, such as orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex, by combining information from small, localized regions of the visual field. These features are general-purpose representations, shared and required across multiple specialized neural pathways, unlike higher-level, task-specific semantic features. This raises the question if such biologically-grounded features arise in ViTs. In this work, we systematically study how orientation selectivity emerges in ViTs by introducing a suite of neuroscience-inspired metrics: representational similarity score (RSS), orientation recruitment score (ORS), and orientation tuning bandwidth to quantify how orientation is encoded in representational geometry and as a function of model depth. Through extensive analysis, we find that: (1) the training paradigm is the strongest determinant of orientation selectivity, with models sharing an objective, peaking at comparable relative depths regardless of scale (2) many units are orientation-selective early in training, with early-to-middle layers recruiting more such units over time, while deeper layers lose selectivity and broaden their tuning toward semantic encoding and (3) our metrics offer a mechanistic heuristic for how many layers to unfreeze for best downstream generalization. Our framework presents a way to track biologically-grounded features during ViT training, probes how desired properties are encoded in transformer representations, and builds a systematic understanding of how ViTs generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [107] [OPD-V: Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation with Modality Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05131)
*Aniri,Jinhe Bi,Peng Liao,Zengjie Jin,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPD-V，一种视觉导向的On-Policy自蒸馏方法，通过正负教师构造模态平衡信任区域来解决多模态大语言模型中模态不平衡问题，在6个基准和4个骨干网络上显著提升推理性能并降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法利用多样化输入源提取特权信息指导自蒸馏，但忽视了模态不平衡这一MLLM推理固有挑战。当文本信息主导生成时，模型无法充分整合多模态输入，导致精心设计的特权信息未被充分利用，限制了OPSD的效果。

Method: 首先通过Zoom-In图像构造正教师、Mask图像构造负教师，两者展现出不同程度的模态不平衡，发现模态平衡本身可作为特权信息。基于此，OPD-V通过正模态平衡Logits边界定义模态平衡信任区域，选择用于自蒸馏的on-policy tokens。

Result: 在6个基准、4个MLLM骨干网络和5种后训练方法上的实验表明，OPD-V一致性地提升了推理性能，同时降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: 模态不平衡是限制OPSD效果的关键因素，将模态平衡作为特权信息引入自蒸馏流程是有效且高效的改进方向。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has become a standard post-training approach for improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing methods draw privileged information from diverse input sources to guide self-distillation. Yet these designs overlook Modality Imbalance, a challenge inherent to MLLM reasoning. When textual information dominates generation, the model cannot fully integrate its multimodal input. Consequently, carefully designed privileged information remains underused, limiting the effectiveness of OPSD. To examine this limitation, we construct a Positive Teacher with the Zoom-In Image and a Negative Teacher with the Mask Image, which exhibit different degrees of Modality Imbalance. Changes in their reasoning correctness and token logits reveal that Modality Balance can itself serve as privileged information. Motivated by this finding, we introduce OPD-V, a visual OPSD paradigm that instantiates such information through the Positive Teacher and Negative Teacher. Positive Modality-Balance Logits Margins define a Modality-Balance Trust Region that selects the on-policy tokens used for self-distillation. Experiments across 6 benchmarks, 4 MLLM backbones, and 5 post-training methods show that OPD-V consistently improves reasoning performance while reducing training cost.

</details>


### [108] [Predicting Brain Morphometry with MT-GNN: Mesh Evolution in Continuous Time with Graph-Based Metric Tensor Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05132)
*Hao Ding,Daniel Semchin,Paul M. Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-GNN模型，通过预测皮层下结构表面的内在几何（第一基本形式/度量张量）来预测形状随时间的演变，利用可微的ARAP求解器将预测度量解码为表面，并在ADNI数据集14个皮层下结构上以每个时间视野均最优的性能超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量先前的扫描预测皮层下结构形状的演变对预后和临床试验富集有重要价值。现有的纵向网格预测器要么通过高维嵌入外推形状轨迹，要么直接回归顶点变形，缺乏对表面内在几何的建模。

Method: 提出MT-GNN：一个单一图网络，基于任意因果多次访问历史和任意预测视野，预测每个顶点的第一基本形式（度量张量），并以傅里叶编码条件化导前时间。预测的度量通过可微的As-Rigid-As-Possible（ARAP）求解器解码为表面，模型以刚性对齐的顶点误差端到端训练。

Result: 在ADNI数据集的14个皮层下结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测视野均表现最佳：平均顶点误差比时间均值降低2.29%（p=6.1×10⁻⁵），在14/14结构上胜过基线，显著优于DCM（-0.19%）和TransforMesh（-0.45%，p=1.2×10⁻⁴），且优势随预测视野增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过重建训练确保解码预测始终是有效表面并持续改善性能。预测表面内在几何而非直接回归顶点变形，是一种更具原理性的形状演变建模方式，在更长的预测视野下优势更明显。

Abstract: Predicting how a subcortical structure's shape will evolve from a few prior scans could support prognosis and clinical-trial enrichment. Existing longitudinal mesh predictors either extrapolate shape trajectories via high-dimensional embeddings or regress vertex deformations directly. We instead predict the surface's intrinsic geometry in continuous time: a single per-structure graph network predicts the future per-vertex first fundamental form (metric tensor) for an arbitrary causal multiple-visit history and an arbitrary prediction horizon, conditioned on a Fourier encoding of the lead time. The predicted metric is decoded into a surface by a differentiable As-Rigid-As-Possible solver, and the model is trained end-to-end on the rigid-aligned vertex error. Training through the reconstruction keeps the decoded prediction a valid surface and consistently improves it. On 14 subcortical structures from the ADNI dataset, the proposed mesh evolution model (MT-GNN) predicts best among the evaluated methods at every horizon ($-2.29\%$ mean vertex error vs. the temporal mean, $p{=}6.1{\times}10^{-5}$, beating it on 14/14 structures), ahead of geodesic shape regression (DCM, $-0.19\%$) and a mesh transformer (TransforMesh, $-0.45\%$; $p{=}1.2{\times}10^{-4}$), with the lead widening as the horizon grows.

</details>


### [109] [SmartMage: Dynamic Modality Orchestration for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05137)
*Yue Zhang,Yingzhao Jian,Yunqiu Xu,Xiaoxiao Sun,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartMage，一个统一的多模态大语言模型（MLLM），通过动态编排异构模态来实现语义感知的3D场景理解。它包含两个核心模块：SMART（语义引导的模态自适应路由）模块根据语义先验、文本-模态对齐和模态质量选择任务相关模态；MAGE（模态感知门控专家）模块利用模态先验引导专家激活，促进多模态推理的适应性专业化。在五个3D场景理解基准上取得SOTA性能，在RGB-only视频理解上也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在处理3D场景理解时通常依赖固定模态组合，忽视了不同查询对模态的不同需求。这种僵化设计会引入不相关模态的语义噪声，同时未能充分利用更信息丰富的模态，导致计算浪费和推理能力被稀释。因此需要一种能够根据查询语义动态选择和编排模态的方法。

Method: SmartMage包含两个关键模块：(1) Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART)模块，利用语义先验、文本-模态对齐和模态质量三个维度来选择任务相关的模态；(2) Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE)模块，利用模态先验引导专家激活，实现多模态推理中的适应性专业化。

Result: SmartMage在五个3D场景理解基准上取得了最先进的性能，在RGB-only视频理解基准上也获得有竞争力的结果。此外，作者构建了诊断基准ScanFacet，将任务划分为细粒度的语义类别，用于分析每种语义类型偏好的模态组合，观察到的模态-语义模式进一步验证了SmartMage的有效性。

Conclusion: SmartMage通过动态模态编排有效解决了3D场景理解中固定模态组合带来的问题，证明了查询相关的模态选择策略能够提升推理性能和计算效率。模态与语义之间的关联模式为进一步理解和优化多模态推理提供了有价值的洞察。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes is fundamental to embodied intelligence, requiring joint reasoning over heterogeneous information from multiple modalities, including visual and geometric cues. However, the relevance of these modalities often varies across queries. Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically rely on fixed modality combinations, overlooking query-dependent modality needs. Such a rigid design can introduce semantic noise from irrelevant modalities while underutilizing more informative ones, leading to wasted computation and diluted reasoning. To address these challenges, this paper proposes SmartMage, a unified MLLM that dynamically orchestrates heterogeneous modalities for semantic-aware 3D scene understanding. Specifically, SmartMage incorporates: (1) a Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART) module that selects task-relevant modalities using semantic priors, text-modality alignment, and modality quality; and (2) a Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE) module that leverages modality priors to guide expert activation, fostering adaptive specialization in multimodal reasoning. Empirically, SmartMage achieves state-of-the-art performance across five 3D scene understanding benchmarks, and attains competitive results on RGB-only video understanding benchmarks. In our diagnostic benchmark ScanFacet, tasks are divided into fine-grained semantic categories, enabling analysis of modality combinations preferred by each semantic type. The observed modality-semantic patterns provide further evidence of SmartMage's effectiveness. Project page: https://yuecheong.github.io/SmartMage/.

</details>


### [110] [Objects as Audio-Visual Modal Sound Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05145)
*Zisen Shao,Zihao Wei,Derong Jin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-MSF，一种基于多视角图像和少量撞击声音记录重建物体级声场的表示方法，通过3D高斯溅射结合密集3D视觉特征，用紧凑的物理模态参数表示撞击声场，实现少样本鲁棒重建，在真实数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D重建主要关注物体几何和外观，忽略了物理交互产生的丰富声学线索。撞击声音能反映材料、刚度和结构属性，但现有的撞击声音建模要么依赖昂贵的物理仿真，要么需要大规模数据集，缺乏高效且通用的解决方案。

Method: AV-MSF基于3D Gaussian Splatting构建，集成密集3D视觉特征作为几何感知先验，并使用紧凑的物理模态参数来表示撞击声场，实现少样本下的鲁棒重建。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AV-MSF在撞击声音渲染上达到最先进水平，超越基于物理仿真和数据驱动的方法，并展示了接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。

Conclusion: AV-MSF证明了将视觉几何先验与物理模态参数结合，能够在少量声音样本下高效重建物体级声场，为声学-视觉联合建模提供了新的方向。

Abstract: While modern 3D reconstruction excels at modeling object geometry and appearance, it largely ignores the rich acoustic cues revealed through physical interaction. Object impact sounds convey material, stiffness, and structural properties that complement vision, yet existing impact sound modeling approaches either rely on expensive physics-based simulation or require large datasets to generalize in a purely data-driven manner. We introduce Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF), a novel object-level acoustic representation reconstructed from multi-view images and only a few impact sound recordings. AV-MSF builds on 3D Gaussian Splatting integrated with dense 3D visual feature to provide a strong geometry-aware prior, and represents the impact sound field using compact, physically meaningful modal parameters, enabling robust few-shot reconstruction. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that AV-MSF achieves state-of-the-art impact sound rendering, outperforming both physics-based and data-driven baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate downstream applications enabled by our representation, including contact localization and object sound editing.

</details>


### [111] [CoCo-IR: Contextual Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05149)
*Shengcao Cao,Tanmaya Shekhar Dabral,Zhongli Ding,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Kaifeng Chen,Zhe Li,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo-IR任务，即上下文组合图像检索，允许用户通过多轮交互逐步细化搜索结果。作者基于大型多模态模型（LMM）构建了一个上下文感知推理模型，生成可跨轮次演化的可变换图像嵌入（TIE），并设计了完全自主、可扩展的数据引擎来生成高质量训练数据。实验表明该方法在CIRCO基准上达到39.4 mAP@5，在CoCo-IR基准上4轮对话R@1达44.1，显著优于无法处理多轮上下文的现有方法（28.2 R@1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于指令的图像检索系统仅限于单轮交互，无法捕捉复杂真实场景中视觉搜索的迭代特性，难以满足用户逐步细化的检索需求。

Method: 提出基于LMM的上下文感知推理模型，通过理解完整交互历史生成可跨轮次演化的Transformable Image Embeddings（TIE）；同时开发完全自主、可扩展的数据引擎，利用LMM生成高质量上下文检索数据，并通过模型引导验证挖掘困难负样本。

Result: 在CIRCO单轮基准上实现39.4 mAP@5的新SOTA表现；在CoCo-IR基准上4轮对话达到44.1 R@1，大幅超越现有方法（28.2 R@1）。

Conclusion: 通过引入CoCo-IR任务和基于LMM的上下文感知模型，验证了多轮交互在图像检索中的有效性，为复杂视觉搜索场景提供了更贴近实际需求的解决方案。

Abstract: Current instruction-based image retrieval systems are powerful but limited to single-turn interactions, failing to capture the iterative nature of complex, real-world visual searches. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contextual Composed Image Retrieval (CoCo-IR), a novel task that enables users to progressively refine search results through interactions. We address this new task by proposing a new model based on a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) that functions as a context-aware reasoner for CoCo-IR. Our model interprets the entire interaction history to generate Transformable Image Embeddings (TIE) that evolve across turns. To fuel the model training without expensive human annotations, we develop a fully autonomous, scalable data engine that leverages LMMs to generate high-quality contextual retrieval data, and uses model-guided verification to mine challenging hard negatives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance: We achieve 39.4 mAP@5 on the challenging single-turn benchmark CIRCO; furthermore, on our new CoCo-IR benchmark, our model maintains robust performance with 44.1 R@1 on 4-turn dialogues, dramatically outperforming existing methods (28.2 4-turn R@1) that fail to handle multi-turn context. Project page: https://CoCo-IR.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Transfer Learning for Named Entity Recognition of Classical Latin through LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04015)
*Callum Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了uOttawa团队在EvaLatin 2026命名实体识别（NER）共享任务中的系统方案与结果。通过使用商业大语言模型（gemini-2.5-pro和claude-sonnet-4-5）进行提示工程，团队在粗粒度（11类）和细粒度（28类）两个NER子任务中均取得第一名，在所有评估指标和严格/宽松评估机制下均获得最佳成绩，展示了跨语言迁移学习对古代拉丁语等低资源语言的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典拉丁语数字资源的日益丰富以及大规模语言模型的突破性进展，为古代语言研究提供了新的机遇。拉丁语作为代表性不足的古代语言，在NLP任务中面临资源匮乏的挑战，作者希望通过利用现代LLM的跨语言迁移能力来弥补这一差距。

Method: 采用商业LLM（gemini-2.5-pro和claude-sonnet-4-5）进行提示工程（prompt engineering），针对EvaLatin 2026的粗粒度NER（11类）和细粒度NER（28类）两个子任务，分别在严格和宽松两种评估机制下进行实验与调优。

Result: 在两个NER子任务中均排名第一，在所有评估指标和评估机制（严格/宽松）下均获得所有参赛队伍中的最高分数，验证了基于LLM提示工程方法在古典拉丁语NER任务上的优越性能。

Conclusion: 古代拉丁语等低资源语言可以通过跨语言迁移学习，受益于更广泛的LLM社区的技术进步。基于商业LLM的提示工程方法在古典拉丁语NER任务上展现了非常强的效果，为该领域的研究提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: With the increase in digitized resources of Classical Latin texts and modern breakthroughs of Large Language Models (LLMs), I contribute to ancient language research by participating in EvaLatin 2026. This paper describes Team uOttawa's system description and results for the Named Entity Recognition (NER) shared task. The task is divided into two subtasks: coarse-grained NER with 11 classes and fine-grained NER with 28 classes, each evaluated under strict and fuzzy regimes. Through prompt engineering of commercial LLMs gemini-2.5-pro and claude-sonnet-4-5, I show that the underrepresented ancient Latin language can take advantage of cross-lingual transfer learning by using advancements made by the wider LLM development community. Overall, the methods discussed in this report demonstrate very strong results, placing first in both NER subtasks and achieving the best scores across all evaluation metrics and regimes among all submissions.

</details>


### [113] [When More Becomes Less: Position-Dependent Repetition Effects in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04021)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现闭式完形填空探测中，重复目标词的数量对预测的影响取决于读出位置：相邻位置呈"启动效应"式单调上升，而位移位置呈倒U型曲线，且该效应在13个模型中均显著，跨4种语言42个多语言组合中可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭式探测隐含假设目标词重复次数对预测的影响与读出槽位位置无关，作者质疑这一假设并设计实验验证。

Method: 设计双探测方案：固定重复目标前缀，仅改变读出位置（相邻探测：紧随重复块之后；位移探测：置于新句框架内）。通过因果消融（六条件）排除长度、冗余度、语义邻居等混淆因素，并通过框架语用控制排除读出框架干扰。

Result: 相邻重复呈单调上升并趋于平台；位移重复呈倒U型，每个词的下降效应在13个开源的编码器和解码器模型中bootstrap置信区间均排除零，42个多语言组合中有42个可复现。注意力分析显示因果LM中每个目标词的注意力随N下降但重复块的总体预算增长，掩码LM则不然。

Conclusion: 重复计数变化的探测实验不能将读出位置视为与测量目标正交的因素；倒U型效应由精确词汇重复驱动，而非长度或语义冗余等因素。

Abstract: Cloze-style probes that vary how often a target token appears implicitly assume that more copies of a target affect prediction the same way regardless of where the readout slot sits. We show this assumption fails. Our two-probe design holds a repeated-target prefix fixed and varies only the readout position: the adjacent probe places the slot immediately after the repeated block; the displaced probe places it inside a fresh sentence frame. Adjacent repetition behaves as priming intuition predicts: $P(\text{target})$ climbs with $N$ and plateaus. Displaced repetition produces an inverted-U: $P(\text{target})$ rises to an early peak and then declines as more copies are added. The displaced inverted-U shows a per-word drop with bootstrap CI excluding zero in all 13 open-access encoder and decoder models we test, and replicates across Spanish, Chinese, German, and French in 42 of 42 multilingual cells. A six-condition causal ablation isolates the effect to exact lexical repetition rather than length, generic redundancy, or semantic-neighbour exposure. A frame-pragmatics control rules out an artefact of the readout frame. Internally, per-target-token attention falls with $N$ while the total budget assigned to the repeated block grows in causal LMs but not in the masked LM we probe. Probes that vary repetition count cannot treat the readout position as orthogonal to what they measure.

</details>


### [114] [Reconstructing Persistent Worlds from Narratives for Narrative-Grounded Interactive Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04037)
*Yi-Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将"从叙事描述中重建显式持久世界"作为叙事驱动交互式体验的核心计算目标，并开发了参考原型，通过三个案例研究证明了该方法在生成可玩环境的同时保持与源叙事的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的叙事规划、场景生成和游戏玩法生成等方法都为特定下游任务构建计算表示，而非显式重建和维护支撑它们的持久世界。叙事驱动的交互式内容生成仍然劳动密集，需要与叙事隐含的世界保持一致。

Method: 作者提出将重建和维持持久实体、位置、语义关系及演化世界状态作为核心目标，仅推断支持连贯交互体验所需的上下文信息。开发了参考原型，从叙事描述重建结构化持久世界表示，并实例化为可玩的瓦片式环境。

Result: 通过程序化场景、原创奇幻叙事和改编公共领域故事三个代表性案例研究，证明了从叙事中重建持久世界的可行性，并展示共享世界表示如何支持连贯游戏玩法且保持对源叙事的扎根。

Conclusion: 显式重建持久世界为AI辅助游戏创作、混合主动性设计、教育模拟和叙事驱动交互体验提供了语义基础，弥合了计算叙事理解与交互式内容生成之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Designing narrative-grounded interactive experiences remains labor-intensive because interactive content must align with the underlying world implied by the narrative. Existing approaches formulate problems such as narrative planning, scene generation, and gameplay generation, each constructing computational representations tailored to specific downstream tasks rather than explicitly reconstructing and maintaining the persistent world that grounds them.
  We investigate reconstructing explicit persistent worlds from narrative descriptions as the central computational objective for narrative-grounded interactive realization. Rather than treating the world as an implicit by-product of downstream generation, our approach reconstructs and maintains persistent entities, locations, semantic relationships, and evolving world states while inferring only the contextual information required to support coherent interactive experiences.
  To investigate this perspective, we develop a reference prototype that reconstructs structured persistent world representations from narrative descriptions and subsequently instantiates playable tile-based environments. Through three representative case studies spanning a procedural scenario, an original fantasy narrative, and an adapted public-domain story, we demonstrate the feasibility of reconstructing persistent worlds and show how a shared world representation supports coherent gameplay while remaining grounded in the source narrative.
  By explicitly reconstructing persistent worlds prior to interactive realization, this work bridges computational narrative understanding and interactive content generation, providing a semantic foundation for AI-assisted game authoring, mixed-initiative design, educational simulations, and narrative-grounded interactive experiences.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Sexism Detection Using Multi-Agent Perspectivist Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04056)
*Hadi Mohammadi,Tina Shahedi,Robert A. Bagheri,Mehdi Dastani,Masoume M. Raeissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP-PO框架，通过按标注行为而非人口统计特征对标注者聚类，并训练多个LLM代理分别模拟各聚类标注行为，同时利用偏好优化结合个体与团队奖励来协调各代理，从而保留文本性别歧视标注中的多元视角分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在标注性别歧视文本时常存在真实的分歧，而大多数NLP系统通过多数投票丢弃了这些分歧。作者认为这些分歧是合理的视角差异而非错误，因此需要保留这些多元视角。

Method: 在EXIST 2024数据集上，首先按标注行为而非人口统计特征对标注者进行聚类；然后为每个聚类微调一个LLM代理以复现该聚类的标注行为；最后通过结合个体与团队级别奖励的偏好优化来协调各代理。实验涵盖两种语言和两种骨干语言模型的四种设置。

Result: 两个关键发现在所有四种设置中一致：一是未经微调的代理行为几乎无差异，聚类特定训练是必要的；二是仅用各聚类自身标签训练代理会导致其偏离应代表的聚类，而加入共享的团队级训练信号能持续使每个代理保持与其聚类校准。

Conclusion: MAP-PO框架能有效保留标注中的多元视角，团队级训练信号对维持代理与对应聚类的校准至关重要，为处理NLP中标注者分歧问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: When people label text for sexism, they often disagree, and not because some of them are wrong: they genuinely perceive sexism differently. Most NLP systems discard this disagreement by collapsing it into a majority vote. We propose the Multi-Agent Perspectivist Preference Optimization (MAP-PO) framework to keep these different perspectives. On the EXIST 2024 dataset of labeled English and Spanish tweets, we first cluster annotators by their labeling behavior rather than their demographic attributes. We then fine-tune one Large Language Model agent per cluster to reproduce that cluster's annotation behavior, and coordinate the agents with preference optimization that combines individual and team-level rewards. We evaluate MAP-PO in four settings defined by two languages and two backbone language models, asking whether each agent reproduces the annotations of its own cluster and whether the agents together reproduce the majority label. Two findings hold in all four settings. First, without fine-tuning the agents behave almost identically, so cluster-specific training is necessary. Second, we show that training each agent only on the labels of its own cluster pushes the agents far beyond the clusters they should represent, while adding a shared team-level training signal consistently keeps each agent calibrated to its cluster.

</details>


### [116] [Mind the Cap: Output-Budget Regimes Change the Measured Multilingual Reasoning Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04160)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示多语言评测中输出token上限是一个被忽视的实验变量——不同语言表达相同内容所需token数不同，单一上限会导致评测结果失真。作者通过系统性实验证明，原生语言与翻译之间的准确率差距在MGSM数据集上随预算变化最多摆动57个点，长度归一化在预算紧张时最多移动38.9个点，且能反转策略排名。冻结的验证测试、词表扩展实验、预算公告效应实验以及正确发射时序恒等式均支持该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评测通常在所有语言上使用同一输出token上限报告准确率，但不同语言表达同一内容所需token数不同，导致上限成为隐藏实验变量，可能使"原生语言vs翻译"的差距产生系统性偏差。

Method: 在MGSM（德语、泰语、斯瓦希里语）上，对Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在四种提示策略下系统扫描输出预算；预先冻结扫描峰值的三个Qwen点进行540,000次独立硬上限解码验证；进行词表扩展适应梯度和公告预算vs强制预算的对照实验；推导正确发射时序恒等式进行交叉验证。

Result: 差距随预算摆动最多57点；长度归一化最多移动38.9点并可反转策略排名；冻结的家庭测试拒绝所有零假设但1024处因原生语言已饱和而无法拒绝；词表扩展在冻结预算关闭0点差距而在19%截断时关闭4.9点；公告预算从128改为2048可使泰语原生准确率移动5.1点；时序恒等式与预估峰值误差仅0.65点。

Conclusion: 输出token上限应被视为独立的实验变量，评测应跨预算区间报告准确率而非单一预算点；饱和后的残余差异反映的是策略性能差距而非推理能力缺陷。

Abstract: Multilingual evaluations report accuracy at a single output-token cap, but languages need different numbers of tokens to express the same content, so the cap is a hidden experimental variable. We test whether the native-vs-translate gap on MGSM (German, Thai, Swahili) is a token-budget artifact for Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four prompting strategies. The measured gap swings by up to 57 points across budgets, length normalization moves it by up to 38.9 points where the cap binds, and at tight caps normalization can reverse which strategy scores higher. We prospectively froze the sweep's three Qwen peaks and its near-zero value at 1024 and evaluated them on 540,000 independently hard-capped decodes: a second frozen family of six Holm-corrected tests rejects every null. The frozen test at $B^*=1024$ still fails to reject because native accuracy has already saturated there; above saturation, the residual difference is a strategy-performance gap, not an identified reasoning deficit. The same truncation channel prices a cost-ordered adaptation ladder: a cross-fitted Thai vocabulary extension closes 0.0 points of the gap at the frozen budget and 4.9 points where 19% of traces still truncate. A third frozen family varies only the announced budget at a fixed enforced cap; announcing 128 rather than 2048 tokens moves Thai native accuracy by 5.1 points, so accuracy is not a function of the enforced cap alone. A correct-emission timing identity computed from one long-cap run matches the three pre-specified MGSM peaks to 0.65 points and, in an exploratory Qwen-only analysis of three further benchmarks, tracks held-out items to 0.92 points, locating the peak exactly in five of seven cells. Treat the output cap as an independent variable and report accuracy across the budget regime, not at a single budget.

</details>


### [117] [Visualizing Graph-to-Answer Mechanism Recovery in Materials-Science Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04170)
*Shashwat Sourav,Subhadeep Pal,Markus J. Buehler,Sanjay Das,Fiona Y. Wang,Dominik Soos,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图到答案的机制追踪诊断工作流，用于分析AI合著科学家模型（Graph-PRefLexOR-8B）在生成材料科学假设时是否保留科学机制。通过语义回溯、图损坏、激活恢复测量和逐层标记网格等多种方法，发现该模型在层7-10的早期过渡区域机制恢复较弱，而机制恢复集中在层30和36的晚期综合与答案起始区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI合著科学家虽然能生成流畅的材料科学假设，但流畅性并不能证明答案保留了科学上有意义的机制。需要一种方法来识别生成假设在何处丢失或恢复机制支持，以避免将其传递给下游实验规划。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，对Graph-PRefLexOR-8B模型（基于Qwen3-8B适应而成，具有脑暴、图构建、模式提取和综合四个阶段）进行机制追踪。综合使用语义回溯、图损坏、基于激活的恢复测量以及按层-标记区域网格的方法，构建可视化诊断工作流。对100个开放式的材料科学问题，在37个残差流检查点（嵌入输出和36个Transformer块）上进行全面扫描。

Result: 最终答案与模型自身结构化阶段（特别是综合阶段）最为接近。在图损坏条件下，早期过渡区域（第7-10层）几乎没有机制恢复，机制恢复反而集中在第30层和第36层附近的晚期综合和答案起始区域。

Conclusion: 该诊断工作流旨在帮助科学家和模型开发者识别生成假设在何处丢失或重新获得机制支持，从而在将其传递给下游实验规划之前进行干预。

Abstract: AI co-scientists can generate fluent materials-science hypotheses, but fluency does not show that an answer preserves a scientifically meaningful mechanism.
